# This is NOT the Next.js you know

This version has breaking changes — APIs, conventions, and file structure may all differ from your training data. Read the relevant guide in `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` (resolved from this file's directory; in monorepos the `next` package may not be visible from the repo root) before writing any code. Heed deprecation notices.

This block is written and re-added by `next dev` — verify at `node_modules/next/dist/server/lib/generate-agent-files.js`. Removing it from a diff only re-creates the uncommitted change; committing it with your work keeps the tree clean.

## UI Change Records (legacy — formerly the Appendices dated block)

- 2026-09-20 — fix(chat): Ask Consuela thread stays visible while thinking + streams survive tab navigation (zero backend changes). Two root causes fixed. (1) `showHero` treated "first user message + isTyping" as a hero state, swapping the whole thread (the user's own bubble included) for a 200px "Thinking…" orb until the first content token — with buffered tool rounds that could be minutes. Now `showHero = !hasRealMessages`; the typing-dots bubble (with the tool `statusLine`) is the thinking affordance under the last message; the hero orb branch is deleted. (2) Chat state was component-local and every tab navigation unmounts /chat, so an in-flight stream's updates landed on a dead component — the reply only appeared after a remount refetched today's PB thread (the "if I change pages then it shows" report). NEW `src/lib/chat-store.ts` (module singleton + `useSyncExternalStore`, same idiom as `calendar-member-snapshot.ts`) owns messages + the live stream (AbortController, watermark, hydration); navigating away interrupts nothing and returning re-attaches instantly. `ensureHydrated()` runs once per page load; remounts do an incremental `since` reconcile; localStorage persistence moved into the store; the page keeps only UI-local state. NEW `tests/unit/chat-store.test.ts` (7) + 2 page regressions + reset seam in all four chat page suites. Verified: chat suites 58/58, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2362 passed (323 files; the 2 uncaught teardown exceptions in `tasks-approve-all` are pre-existing — reproduce in isolation), `next build` clean. Contracts in the 2026-09-20 UI Change Record.

- 2026-09-20 — fix(tasks,pb,hall): guest completions + week champions. (1) /api/tasks/claim + /api/members/verify middleware-exempt (PIN is the credential); NEW server-authoritative `complete`/`undo` actions on the claim route (idempotent, reversal-aware, kid completions land pendingApproval); Tasks page assigned-completion + undo persist through them. (2) hall_of_fame.emoji 5000-char cap silently rejected every photo-avatar enshrinement — seed now declares max 400000 (self-healed live) + NEW hall-of-fame-backfill module recomputes champions from week_archive inside GET /api/tasks/sync (idempotent, celebration-safe, older weeks pre-celebrated). Live-verified: guest complete → 200 + snapshot + week_data; Aurora's 2026-09-07 win enshrined with prize text; June week recovered as history. Ops: pb:seed run (emoji heal landed).

- 2026-09-19 — feat(tasks): Open & Crew Tasks — full plan Tasks 2–16 (the earlier partial landing `6a6db2f` had only the crew fields). Aligned the model to the approved spec (`crew: { members:[{name,emoji,joinedAt,checkedInAt?}], removed? }`, `pendingApproval.crew`), fixed the PB `0`-coercion merge churn, added crew helpers + union merge with tombstones, crew-join/check-in/remove server actions (keyed lock; set-once check-ins; last-slot 409; PIN-free session path for under-10s), open-task speed bonus, crew approval paying each member full points (taskId+member idempotency, reversal-aware), the Assigned/Open/Crew modal + Open board on the Tasks page, parent crew management, KidHome crew join/check-in, and open/crew AI suggestions (validator + mapper). Ops: `npm run pb:seed`. Verified: tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2268 passed / 2 failed (both pre-existing TZ-streak), `next build` clean. Contracts in the 2026-09-19 UI Change Record.

- 2026-09-18 — fix(shop): mobile grocery rows squeezed product names to ~10px — actions collapsed into a ⋯ overflow sheet. Root cause (measured live at 390×844): ListRow's title (`min-w-0 flex-1 truncate`) always yields to the `shrink-0` trailing cluster (StorePill 40-47px + priority Chip ~62px + 44px lock/edit/delete + gaps ≈ 256-294px), leaving ~10px for the name on phones — emoji visible, name gone. Progressive regression (store pills 2026-08-31; 44px targets earlier); desktop (xl `1fr_280px` grid) unaffected. Fix (commits 8324694, 9eadb5a, 089be56, 6bb1db6, 1293c65): NEW `src/components/meals/GroceryItemActionsSheet.tsx` (shared-Modal sheet — 🥫 Send to pantry (checked-only, `pantryBusy`-gated) / 📌 Lock/Unlock auto-sync (flips on `manualOverride`) / ✎ Edit details / 🗑️ Delete (rose)) + `ShopTab.tsx` trailing restructure (StorePill visible at ALL sizes; Pantry + Chip + lock + edit + delete UNCHANGED inside `hidden sm:flex`; `sm:hidden` ⋯ with `aria-label="More actions for {name}"`; split `actionsItemId`/`actionsSheetItem` state keeps the sheet's item alive through the 150ms Modal exit; checkbox wrapper + ⋯ `h-11`→`h-9` + `.hit-44` = 36px visual / 44px effective); priority Chip phones-hidden BY USER DECISION (not in the sheet). Verified: probe `scripts/consuela/verify-shop-mobile-actions.mjs` **34/34 ALL CHECKS PASSED** (390×844 + 1280×800, dark + light, guest — titles ≥140px, measured 141/148/148px; full names; no overflow; sheet flows; desktop untouched), NEW suites `grocery-item-actions-sheet` (5) + `shop-tab-mobile-actions` (5), `grocery-handoff`/`grocery-override-ui` green, tsc clean, eslint 0 on the 4 touched files, full suite 2239 passed / 2 failed (308 files — only the 2 documented pre-existing TZ-streak failures; the initially-reported full-suite-only `shop-tab-mobile-actions` failures were a harness race — the sheet's 150ms Modal closing timer firing into a `document.body` wiped by the next test — resolved by unmounting the test root in `afterEach`, which clears the timer before the wipe). Contracts in the 2026-09-18 UI Change Record. Ops: none (no PB schema/migration).

- 2026-09-18 — fix(chat): Ask Consuela thread ordering + clean new conversation. Root causes: (1) the mount PB hydration did a blind `setMessages(pbMsgs)` replace, so a send that raced the initial fetch was dropped and re-appended at the end by the post-send reconcile → reply above request; (2) `/new` appended a divider but the render mapped the full `messages` array, so history never cleared; (3) `mergePBThread` keyed dedupe on `role:speaker:content`, leaving a duplicate for signed-out rows. Fix (TDD): NEW pure `src/lib/chat-thread.ts` (`threadKey` role+content; `sortThread` by `Message.at` with user→divider→reply ties; additive `mergeThread`; `visibleThread` slices at the newest `system` marker and drops the seed greeting once a user row exists); both hydration effects merge instead of replace; `visibleMessages` drives the thread map + hero/quick-actions/read-aloud; `showHero` hands over once any real message exists; `selectChatMessages` sorts `"createdAt,id"`; confirm copy + journey updated. Tests: NEW `chat-thread` (18) + delayed-hydration ordering page test; chat suites 42/42; full suite 2228 passed / 3 failed (all 3 stash-proven pre-existing on HEAD: TZ-streak ×2 + weather-strip ×1); tsc clean; eslint 0 on touched files. Contracts in the 2026-09-18 UI Change Record. Ops: none (no PB schema/migration).

- 2026-09-18 — fix(home): Home "Today" widget never merged Google Calendar events. Root cause: `src/app/page.tsx` `refreshTodayEvents` read only the family `events` collection via `db.selectTodaysEvents()`; Google rows (`consuela_google_calendar_events`) were merged only on the Calendar page / chat tools / planner / screensaver (the 2026-09-15 multi-day fix's scope), so Google-only events like today's all-day "Bailey & Emily at home!" never appeared on Home. Fix (TDD red→green): the widget now fetches `/api/google-calendar` and merges rows covering today via the shared `googleEventCoversDay` contract + `mapGoogleEvent` (per-calendar `colorHex` honored by `eventLineColor`; all-day rows first, then by time; Events stat tile inherits the count; fetch failure/401 gracefully keeps family rows). Tests: NEW `tests/unit/home-today-google-events.test.tsx` (4). Verified: full suite 2205/2207 (2 failures = documented pre-existing TZ-streak, stash-proven), tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean, live Playwright probes 11/11 (guest fallback, signed-in payload contains the Bailey row, real-browser render of the card rows + counts + ended-yesterday exclusion). Contracts in the 2026-09-18 UI Change Record. Ops: none (deploy picks it up; no pb:seed).

Diagnosis: an external agent that logged in before the toggle was enabled kept a non-admin token, saw the 47-tool catalog, and described the surface as restricted. Docs/comments updated (`docs/muse-api.md` catalog note + invariant, `tools/route.ts`, `execute.ts`, `token.ts`, AGENTS §2.3/§5.6). Tests: `muse-authorize` live-semantics case table + `muse-tools-route` +2 cross-cases; `muse-*` suites 65/65. No schema/ops change.

(2) Kid write-tool gates: `/api/consuela/suggestions/act` 403 `adult_only` for child/pet PINs on the 6 write tools (reads + `dismiss_suggestion` still any-member); the Clem branch of `/api/hermes/chat` now passes the session-derived `role` to `buildToolsForOpenAI` — child/pet/guest Clem sessions arm only the 4 read grocery tools (previously the CLEM filter bypassed the kid allowlist). (4) Both alert crons: keyed lock around load→decide→push→save + NEW `ha/alert-state.ts` CAS save (expected-prev, the setState idiom) — overlapping runs pushed every alert twice before. (5) hall_of_fame: UNIQUE `(member, weekStart)` index in pb-seed + `dedupeHallOfFameRows` pre-index heal (keeps celebrated, else earliest) + `insertHallOfFameEntry` upsert that never loses `celebrated:true`. (6) `mergeTasksSnapshot` dedupes within the snapshot (id AND title). (7) `/api/tasks/sync` non-parent leg-merge under the keyed lock — the stale parent-legs resurrection race is closed. - 2026-09-02 — fix(meals,dates): Consuela-logged meals land on the family's real days (UTC/Eastern resolution bug fixed end-to-end). **Root cause (systematic, live-PB verified):** dashboard container runs UTC (no `TZ` in `docker-compose.yml`) while family is America/Detroit, so "today" flips a day early ~8 PM Eastern; the Hermes system prompt had no current date; and read tools reported the UTC weekday (`src/lib/hermes-tools.ts` `todayISO()` + `get_dashboard_summary`). **Fixes (TDD):** `src/lib/local-date.ts` +`familyTimeZone`/`localWeekdayShort`/`weekdayOfISO`/`localWeekStartISO`/`localDateContext` (+`tests/unit/local-date-context.test.ts`); NEW `src/lib/consuela-prompts.ts` (extracted system prompts + `buildConsuelaSystemPrompt`/`buildClemSystemPrompt`/`buildDateContextBlock` appending "Today is {weekday}, {date} ({tz}) / Yesterday was…", route.ts thread-id `todayISO()` kept UTC per §3.5) (+`tests/unit/consuela-prompts.test.ts`); `hermes-tools.ts` — `todayISO()` now local, `get_dashboard_summary` matches meals by local weekday + reports `today_weekday`/`family_timezone`, `get_weekly_meals` reports `today`/`current_week_monday` (+`tests/unit/hermes-tools-dates.test.ts`); NEW `add_meal` tool (day=weekday short or YYYY-MM-DD, upserts day+mealType+week); `docker-compose.yml` +`TZ=America/Detroit`; data repair script `scripts/consuela/fix-meal-days-2026-09-02.mjs` (moved Little Caesars Pizza Wed→Tue 9/1, Pizza Leftovers Thu→Wed 9/2; 3 sibling rows left alone per user; `from`-guard enforced). Verified: typecheck clean, full suite 796/796, eslint clean on touched files.

- 2026-09-01 — fix(settings): Instacart row showed a misleading red "Not configured" while ordering actually works via Composio. The Settings → Services & Keys card has separate Instacart and Composio rows; the Instacart row required a direct `INSTACART_API_KEY` (which the family doesn't have — Instacart's Developer Platform is closed to new applications), so it read red even though the Shop tab orders fine through the green Composio row. Fix (TDD): `src/lib/services/registry.ts` — `INSTACART_API_KEY` is now `required: false` with help text "Instacart works automatically through Composio…"; `src/lib/services/tests.ts` — `testInstacart` now reports `{ok:true, detail:"connected via Composio"}` when no direct key is set but a Composio key exists (still tests `connect.instacart.com` directly when a `keys.…` key IS present, still `not_configured` when neither). `src/components/settings/ServicesKeysCard.tsx` — `testedMap` now carries `{ok, detail}` so the dot label shows the live detail ("connected via Composio" green) instead of a generic "Test passed". New/updated tests in `services-test-fns.test.ts` (+3: via-Composio, direct-key, neither). Verified live on NAS: instacart field `required=false`, test `ok=true detail=connected via Composio`, order POST still returns a shopping-list URL. Typecheck clean, 705/706 (1 pre-existing hermes-port test), 0 new lint.

- 2026-09-01 — fix(composio,instacart): Composio key stored but unreadable — honest connection dots + Instacart order flow fixed. **Root cause (4 compounding bugs):** (1) the Composio key Jeffery saved to PB on 2026-08-29 was encrypted with a CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY that matches neither local dev nor the live NAS container (container was recreated 2026-08-31 with a different key) → `decryptSecret` silently failed → env fallback empty → route returned honest 503 "Instacart integration is not enabled." (2) `createShoppingListViaComposio` in `src/lib/instacart.ts` parsed the wrong response shape — real v3.1 returns `{"data":{"url":...}}` but code looked for `data.result.output.shopping_list_url` → even with a valid key the function threw "Composio did not return a shopping list URL." (3) The Composio Test button in Settings probed a dead v1 endpoint (`/api/v1/actions` → 410 Gone) which 410 < 500 → the old `bearerReachable` treated 410 as "reachable" → **green dot on an invalid/missing key**. (4) The local `.env.local` and NAS container had different `CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY` values (split-brain) — any secret saved from either environment was unreadable by the other. **Fixes (TDD):** `src/lib/instacart.ts` — `createShoppingListViaComposio` now parses the real v3.1 shape `data?.data?.url` (with legacy fallback) + surfaces Composio's `error.message` on `successful:false`; `src/lib/services/tests.ts` — new dedicated `testComposio()` probing `v3.1/tools?limit=1` (200=green, 401/403/410=red, not green); `src/lib/services/config.ts` — `ServiceFieldStatus` gains `unreadable?: boolean`; `readStoredRow` refactored to `readStoredRowDetailed` returning `{value, raw, unreadable}` so `getServiceStatus` can surface a DB row that exists but can't be decrypted; `src/components/settings/ServicesKeysCard.tsx` — `dotTone` shows "Saved — unreadable (re-enter)" for broken secrets, auto-runs all service tests on card mount (progressive dot updates via `Promise.allSettled`), "Test all" button to refresh, unreadable fields show a rose warning + "Re-enter key" placeholder. **Ops:** re-stored the Composio key via the NAS dashboard PUT (encrypted with the container's correct key); aligned local `.env.local` CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY to match the NAS container's key (prevents future split-brain for the only two encrypted-service rows: composio + any future secrets). **Verified live:** composio test `ok=true key accepted (200)`, Instacart POST returns `https://customers.dev.instacart.tools/store/shopping_lists/19267256...`. New tests: `tests/unit/services-composio-test.test.ts` (5: v3.1 endpoint, 401=red, 410=red, 2xx=green, short-circuit); `tests/unit/composio-instacart.test.ts` (+3: v3.1 `data.url` shape, legacy compat, error surface, `successful:false`); `tests/unit/services-config-resolver.test.ts` (+2: unreadable without env, unreadable with env). Typecheck clean, 702/703 suite (1 pre-existing hermes port test from uncommitted work), eslint clean on touched files.

- 2026-08-31 — fix(profile): photo avatars couldn't save — PocketBase `members.emoji` capped at 5000 chars. Root cause: this PB build treats a text field's `max: 0` as the built-in 5000-char default, so every real photo save (a 256px webp data URL, ~8–60KB base64) hit PB `validation_max_text_constraint` → the profile route 500'd `Failed to update profile`. `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` — `members.emoji` schema now defines `options: { max: 400000 }` (was no options → PB applied its 5000-char default even though the field schema reported `max: 0`), and `seedCollections()` now heals **field-option drift** for text fields the seed gives an explicit `max` — existing members fields with `emoji.max` below 400000 are patched on re-seed; the live NAS PB was healed via `npm run pb:seed` (`members (patched +1 field options (emoji))`). `src/app/api/members/profile/route.ts` — `MAX_AVATAR_CHARS` now exported (same 400_000 value) so the route and seed caps stay in lockstep (guard-tested). New `pb_migrations/1788223651_updated_members.js` (emoji.max 0→400000, revert included). New `tests/unit/pb-seed-field-heal.test.ts` (4 tests: schema carries 400000, seed heals a live 0-max field, already-correct field untouched, route cap == seed max). Root cause verified live: a 2,887-char data URL saved, 5,000+ returned 500; after the fix 250,000-char payloads return 200 and a real noisy photo (50,751-char webp) saves end-to-end with `Saved ✓` and persists to PB. Suite 690/690 (was 661 + 4 new + 25 from other work), tsc clean, eslint clean on touched files.

- 2026-08-31 — feat(weather): widget stays live — 15-min auto-refresh + tab-wake refresh (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-31-weather-live-refresh-design.md). `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — mount-only fetch extracted into `loadWeather()` (`useCallback` on lat/lon, `inFlightRef` overlap guard) and now also runs every 15 min (`setInterval`) and on `visibilitychange` when the tab returns visible with `updatedAt` > 10 min old (`updatedAtRef` mirrors the success timestamp). Silent refresh: `loading` only ever shows before first data; background failure keeps stale data with no banner (`.catch` gated on `dataLoadedRef`) and retries next cycle. The mount effect resets `dataLoadedRef`/`updatedAtRef` when the weather location changes, so a new location's failed first fetch shows the banner (no silent old-city data). Modal auto-live via shared `weatherData`. `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` +7 (poll, stale/fresh wake, silent in-flight swap, failure-keeps-data, location-change banner reset, overlap guard). Verified: typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files, suite 661/661.

- 2026-08-31 — fix(tasks): guest "vanishing tasks" made honest — sign-in empty state replaces the silent empty list. Investigation (live curl + Playwright against the NAS) proved PocketBase never lost the data — all 7 chores created 17:30–17:41 UTC persisted in `tasks` + `consuela_data_snapshots`; the vanishing was the read path: signed-out (incognito/fresh) browsers get 401 on every sessioned read (`/api/tasks/sync`, `/api/db/*`), so the Tasks page fell back to empty local cache and rendered "🎉 All caught up / No pending tasks right now" — indistinguishable from a genuinely empty chore list. Fix (TDD): `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` — the mount restore records `guestSyncBlocked` when the snapshot read 401s, and the Pending empty state swaps to an honest 🔐 EmptyState ("Tasks are synced to the family account / Sign in with your PIN to see everyone's tasks. Your chores aren't gone — they're waiting on the family server.") when `!isLoggedIn && guestSyncBlocked && tasks.length === 0`; signed-in empty lists and guests with local tasks keep the existing copy. New `tests/unit/tasks-guest-sync-state.test.tsx` (3 tests: guest-401 shows the hint, signed-in-empty keeps "All caught up", local tasks render without the hint). Verified: suite 693/693, typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files.
- 2026-08-31 — fix(ops,auth): NAS deploy un-broken — Clem chat + TheMealDB recipe search/save. Three root causes (systematic debugging): (1) container on wrong Docker network (`bridge` instead of `familydashboard_consuela-net`) → `hermes-agent-2` unresolved (Clem chat dead) + `192.168.0.28:8090` unreachable (`ConnectTimeoutError` in container logs → recipe search 502, gateway writes 502); (2) Secure session cookie over plain HTTP (`secure: NODE_ENV === "production"` in the login route) → browsers never sent the cookie on http://192.168.0.28:3000 → every gated write 401'd ("can't save recipes"); (3) my raw-curl recipe POST bypassed `db.upsertRecipe`'s array JSON-stringify — not a bug (UI path verified stringifies). Fixes: recreated container on the shared network with `NEXT_PUBLIC_PB_URL=http://pocketbase:8090`, `HERMES_API_URL=http://hermes-agent-2:8642`, `SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=false`; code change `0a8a75c` adds exported `sessionCookieSecure()` honoring `SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=false` (+3 tests, suite 659/659) and `docker-compose.yml` gained `SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=${…:-false}`. `DEPLOY_NAS_LOCAL.md` gotchas #11–13. Verified live: PB + Hermes reachable from the container; login sets a non-Secure cookie; gateway recipe save 200 and persists full ingredients/tags; Playwright E2E (signed in) — 25 chicken search results, detail + Save to catalog lands in PB, Clem FAB opens and Hermes replies.

- 2026-08-26 — fix(meals): /meals member-strip hydration mismatch (server PB-warmed members cache vs client fallback) + signed-in custom avatars now actually land in the strip. **Root cause (systematic):** the "Hydrate once" IIFE in `src/db/index.ts` warms `membersCache` at module load — on the server it reads PB directly (admin access), so SSR of the PlanTab "Who's eating tonight" strip rendered the real PB members (live PB stores Jeffery's customized emoji 😎), while the client's first render still had an empty cache (the browser roster fetch `/api/members/admin` is async and 401s for guests) and rendered the static fallback (Jeffery 👨 from `src/lib/member-fallback.ts`) → React hydration error `+ 👨 / - 😎` at `AnimatedEmoji`. The 2026-06-13 `mounted` gate only protected `selectMembersDetailed()`; the pre-mount branch still read `db.selectMembers()`, which stopped being server/client-deterministic when the PB warm-up landed. **Fix:** (1) `src/db/index.ts` — new `mappedMembersFallback()` + `db.selectMembersFallback()` (the exact empty-cache mapping, but NEVER reflects the warmed cache); `selectMembers()` reuses it. (2) `src/components/meals/PlanTab.tsx` — pre-mount branch now reads `db.selectMembersFallback()`, so SSR HTML and client hydration are byte-identical (deterministic fallbacks; real roster swaps in after mount). (3) `src/db/index.ts` IIFE now dispatches `consuela-members-updated` on the client after a **successful** roster fetch only (a fallback-only cache must not fire it — `useAuth.handleMembersUpdated` treats a missing member as deleted and signs out; `refreshMembersCache` already follows this rule). (4) `PlanTab` listens for the event and bumps a `membersVersion` recompute trigger on the `familyMembers` memo — previously the `[mounted]`-only memo froze at whatever the cache held the moment mount flipped, so signed-in users never saw their custom avatars in the strip (the roster lands ~100ms later). **Tests (+3, TDD red→green):** `tests/unit/plan-tab-hydration.test.tsx` (node env — mocks `withAdmin` so the server-path hydrate warms the cache with the live PB roster incl. 😎; asserts `selectMembersFallback()` stays deterministic and `renderToString(<PlanTab/>)` contains 👨 and NOT 😎) and `tests/unit/plan-tab-members-swap.test.tsx` (jsdom — gates the roster fetch, asserts fallbacks first then 😎 after `consuela-members-updated`, pets excluded). **Verified:** typecheck clean; suite 592/592 (79 files); eslint 0 on touched files; Playwright guest + signed-in (Rebecca) at 390px — 0 hydration console errors on `/meals` and all three legacy deep links (`?tab=grocery/pantry/recipes` still land on Shop/Stock/Recipe box), signed-in strip shows Jeffery's real 😎 after mount, guest strip shows the honest fallback family. Note: the 2 remaining adult console 403s are the pre-existing `useAuth` legacy direct-PB `auth_sessions` reads (documented fail-soft under PB lockdown) — unrelated.
- 2026-08-26 — feat(kitchen): Kitchen redesigned as one Plan → Shop → Stock loop (plan docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-26-kitchen-plan-shop-stock.md, commits 79508b3..ed4e204). `/meals` rewired from four parallel tabs to a three-step loop — 🍽️ Plan / 🛒 Shop / 🥫 Stock in one `SegmentedControl` radiogroup with a shared collapsible `KitchenFlowCard` stepper (`aria-current="step"` on the active step; collapse persisted to localStorage). New: `KitchenFlowCard.tsx`, `SyncPreviewSheet.tsx` (preview-before-commit modal), `src/lib/kitchen-tabs.ts` (legacy `?tab=` deep-link mapping: grocery→Shop, pantry→Stock, recipes→Plan + Recipe box, meals→Plan), pure `MealSyncService.previewMealPlanToGrocery` / `previewPantryToGrocery`. Renames: MealsTab→PlanTab (inline Recipe box, ghost-member fix, direct sync button removed), RecipesTab→RecipeBox (folded into Plan), GroceryTab→ShopTab (Buy-again row, preview-first meal sync, 44px targets), PantryTab→StockTab (preview-first low/out sync, declutter, async add fix). Backend/hooks/sync engine unchanged. New tests: kitchen-tabs (4), kitchen-flow-card (4), meal-sync-preview (8), sync-preview-sheet (4); grocery-handoff + grocery-override-ui re-pointed at ShopTab. Task 10 verification gate: typecheck clean; lint 0 errors on plan-touched files (KitchenFlowCard `set-state-in-effect` fixed via `useSyncExternalStore` mounted pattern — repo-wide 48 pre-existing errors remain, none plan-touched); suite 589/589 (78 files); build clean; Playwright guest 390px loop 9/9 (deep links, custom meal → preview → Shop list, check-off → bulk send → undo, pantry Low → preview → Shop list, no horizontal overflow). Pre-existing concerns recorded (baseline-verified, not regressions): CapsuleNav overlaps inline-Modal footers (PageShell main z-10 stacking), category-`other` grocery items invisible in ShopTab, one /meals hydration mismatch per guest load (server PB-warmed members cache vs client fallback in the PlanTab member strip).
- 2026-08-26 — fix(kitchen): grocery→pantry handoff works for guests + expired sessions — local-first kitchen writes. Root cause (systematic): the 2026-08-24 session-auth migration gated `/api/db/*` behind the httpOnly `consuela_session` cookie, but `usePantry`/`useGrocery` still gated UI state on PB write results — for guests (and expired sessions) every write 401'd, `addPantryItem` returned false, and `GroceryTab.sendCheckedToPantry` counted all items as failed and kept the checked rows stuck ("Sent 0 to pantry (0 already stocked, 5 failed — kept on list)"). Compounding: live PB `grocery_list_items.userId` was `required: true` (legacy migration field, absent from the seed) while the client create paths never sent it — signed-in grocery creates 400'd at PB too. Fixes: `src/hooks/usePantry.ts` — `addPantryItem` is now local-first (PB write attempted best-effort in try/catch; the `PantryItem` is built from local inputs with `saved?.id ?? Date.now()`, state always updates, item always returned); `updatePantryStatus`/`removePantryItem` state-first; `normalizeName(p.item || "")` guards; PB rows lacking both `name` and `item` filtered on load. `src/hooks/useGrocery.ts` — `addGroceryItem` wraps the upsert in try/catch (local append unchanged); `toggleGroceryNeeded`/`updateGroceryItem`/`toggleManualOverride` optimistic state then best-effort write; `deleteGroceryItem` state-first and passes `item.name` — `src/db/index.ts` `deleteGroceryItem(id, name?)` falls back to gateway list + delete-by-name when the id delete fails (local numeric fallback ids don't exist in PB; the rows a toggle materializes are now cleaned up instead of resurrecting). `src/db/index.ts` + `src/db/pb-db.ts` — grocery create defaults `{ userId: "demo", ...data }`; `src/lib/hermes-tools.ts` chat grocery create sends `userId: "demo"`; `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` — `grocery_list_items` seed gains optional `userId`. Live PB patched via admin API: `userId.required` → false; all test/probe rows removed (grocery 0, pantry 0). Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 2 pre-existing unused-disable warnings; suite 569/569; Playwright guest scenario (all writes 401) — toast "🥫 Sent 5 items to pantry", checked rows clear, pantry tab shows the items from local state; signed-in scenario — 5× POST pantry 200, numeric-id deletes 502 then name-fallback deletes 200, PB grocery ends 0 rows / pantry 5.
- 2026-08-26 — fix(calendar): Google-synced events now month-accurate (no ghost events, no dropped months). Root cause: `syncGoogleEvents` in `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` mapped Google events to day-of-month only (`day: dayNum`) AND filtered them to the current month at sync time, while the grid/panels keyed events by day number for every displayed month — so current-month Google events ghosted onto the same day numbers in all other months, and next-month events never appeared when navigated to. Backend audited clean (`src/lib/google/calendar.ts` stores real `start_iso` across a 30d-back/90d-forward window; `/api/google-calendar` returns all cached rows). Fix: NEW `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts` — `parseGoogleStart` (all-day date-only parsed as local dates, preserving the existing US-timezone shift fix), `mapGoogleEvent` (keeps day+month+year; id `g_{google_id}_{day}_{month}_{year}_{time}`), `eventInMonth` (no month/year = repeats every month, preserving family-event behavior). `CalEvent` gained optional month/year; sync keeps the full window (no current-month filter), dedupe includes month/year; `dayEventMap`/`selectedEvents`/`upcomingByDay` filter via `eventInMonth`. Tests: NEW `tests/unit/calendar/google-mapping.test.ts` (9, TDD red→green). Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect; suite 569/569; Playwright probe with mocked sync (Aug 12/28 + Sep 3/12 events) — August shows 12+28, September shows 3+12 with day 28 clean, July shows no Google ghosts, family repeats intact, no page errors.
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): Calendar ⇄ Schedule tabs — solid accent active pill. `src/app/globals.css` — `.calendar-tab.is-active` was a 24% accent wash + primary text that blended into the glass track (hard to tell which tab was highlighted); now a solid accent gradient (`--color-accent-button` → 76% accent mixed with `#111827`), white text, inset top highlight, accent glow shadow via `color-mix`; added `.calendar-tab.is-active:hover` so hover doesn't dim the white label; inactive tabs unchanged (muted text, transparent). Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect; suite 560/560; detector 8 unchanged; Playwright confirms solid gradient + white text in dark and light and the active pill follows the tap.
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): selected-today keeps both signals — clear translucent box + today circle. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — day-cell className was an either/or ternary (`is-selected` XOR `is-today`), so today's marker vanished whenever today was selected; now independent flags (`is-selected` AND `is-today`). `src/app/globals.css` — today circle un-scoped from `:not(.is-selected)` (always renders on today); selected today gains a 2px white ring on the circle; new `.calendar-day-btn.is-selected.is-today` swaps the dense gradient for a clear translucent accent box (20% fill dark / 22% light via `color-mix`, 60%+white accent border, inset white ring, softer glow, scale 1.06 kept) with matching overrides in both light blocks (`:root[data-theme="light"] .is-today` outranks the base rule); event dots in selected-today use accent color to stay visible on the tint. Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect; suite 560/560; detector 8 unchanged; Playwright confirms both classes + solid circle + translucent fill (no gradient) when today is selected, and circle-on-today + gradient-on-selection when another day is selected, in dark and light.
- 2026-08-26 — fix(ui): console hygiene — theme script via next/script + Calendar hydration mismatch fixed. `src/app/layout.tsx` — anti-FOUC theme-init script moved from raw `<script dangerouslySetInnerHTML>` (React 19 dev warning "Encountered a script tag while rendering React component") to `next/script strategy="beforeInteractive"` (Next.js 16 app-router pattern: injected into server HTML head, runs pre-hydration; verified data-theme set at domcontentloaded in dark + light). `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — fixed "Hydration failed… server rendered HTML didn't match the client" in the month-grid day dots: `calEvents`/`schedules` lazy-init from localStorage on the client (stored events) while the server rendered fallbacks, so dot counts/event rows differed at hydration. Added a `mounted` flag via `useSyncExternalStore(subscribeNoop, clientTrue, serverFalse)` (no effect/setState — the strict `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` rule flags the classic mounted-effect when the flag feeds memo cascades) + `renderEvents`/`renderSchedules` memos serving deterministic fallbacks until mount; `dayEventMap`/`selectedEvents`/`upcomingByDay`/`sortedSchedules` + routines count/empty-state render sites read the gated values; state/CRUD/write-back effects unchanged. Verified: typecheck clean; eslint back to the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect only; suite 560/560; Playwright probes — zero hydration/script-tag console errors in dark + light, including a repro with stored events differing from fallbacks (dots render correctly after mount); detector findings unchanged (8 pre-existing).
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): Calendar month grid adopts Apple Calendar signatures. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — grid header restructured: month title left + `.calendar-month-nav` group (prev chevron ‹ Today pill › next chevron); today's date number gains `is-today-number`. `src/app/globals.css` — today's number renders as a 23px filled accent circle (white numeral + accent-matched glow, scoped `:not(.is-selected)`); `.is-today` tile tint softened (14%→8% dark / 12%→7% light) and re-derived from `--color-accent-selected` via `color-mix` (was `--calendar-accent-rgb` rgba that could mismatch the circle under non-blue accents); `.calendar-month-title` 1.05rem/800 → 1.3rem/600 with muted year; `.calendar-today-btn` full 999px pill; new ghost chevrons (borderless round 36px, dark + light hover); `.calendar-day-dot` 3.2px → 4.2px. Verified: typecheck clean; 0 new lint (1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect unchanged); suite 560/560; Playwright probes confirm circle (23.2px, 999px radius, accent fill, matching glow) + nav order + pill radius + dot size in dark and light, no page errors, no overflow.

Verified: Home `.widget-card` vs Calendar grid card computed styles byte-identical in dark and light; typecheck clean; 0 new lint (1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect unchanged); suite 560/560; Playwright probes clean (no page errors, no horizontal overflow at 390/1440, both tabs + open form).
- 2026-08-26 — fix(backend): audit follow-up — regression tests for input route guards + dead demo cleanup. Added `tests/unit/input-route-guards.test.ts` (5 tests: OCR/voice/photo multipart guards, email-forward validation, email-forward `members` lookup + missing `consuela_saved_locations` fallback); red-green verified by stashing the route fixes (4 failures) and restoring them (5 passes). Deleted dead phantom-collection demo modules: `src/lib/family-ai.ts`, `src/lib/hermes-tools-enhanced.ts`, `src/lib/morning-briefing.ts`, old `src/hooks/useMorningBriefing.ts`, `src/components/morning-briefing/`, `src/components/family-ai/`. Fixed `src/db/features/seed.ts` `users`→`members`. Verified suite 560/560, `tsc` clean, eslint clean on touched files, live route probes unchanged.
- 2026-08-26 — fix(backend): comprehensive backend audit — gateway unblocked (autodate fields), unauthenticated-PB reads fixed, dead/dangerous routes removed. Full sweep of all 78 API routes (safe probes: empty bodies / missing auth / fake ids; side-effect routes avoided) surfaced a second class of breakage. **(1) Gateway unblocked — the biggest win:** NO PB collection had the standard `created`/`updated` autodate fields, but the sessioned gateway `/api/db/[collection]` defaults to `sort=-created`, so EVERY browser-side gateway read 400'd at PB ("Something went wrong…") → bare empty-body 500 → the dual-mode client silently fell back to stale local cache (cross-device sync dead for all 19 allowlisted collections). Fix in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts`: new `withAutodate()` injects `created`/`updated` autodate fields into every collection and a shared `buildField()` now drives both the create and schema-patch paths (autodate emits `onCreate`/`onUpdate`, no `required`); re-ran `npm run pb:seed` → all collections patched +2 fields. Gateway routes hardened to return parseable JSON instead of empty 500s: `src/app/api/db/[collection]/route.ts` + `[id]/route.ts` wrap PB calls in try/catch → `db_error` 502, and bad JSON bodies → `invalid_json` 400. **(2) Unauthenticated-PB reads fixed:** several server libs still used the public `getPB()` client, which 403s on every locked collection. New `getAuthedPB()` in `src/lib/pb-auth.ts` (same cached admin client + token refresh as `withAdmin`) for call sites that read more naturally with a `pb` handle. Switched: `skill-tree.ts` (GET /api/skill-tree was 500 "Failed to load skill tree data" → now 200, creates/reads real profiles), `schedule-analytics.ts` (was 500 "Only superusers can perform this action." → now 200), `recurring-patterns.ts` (collections already seeded → now returns real data), `family-memory.ts` (its `consuela_family_memories` collection was never seeded → added as the 37th collection in `pb-seed.ts`, now create/read/delete round-trips). **(3) formData + email routes:** `ocr/extract`, `voice/process`, `photo/process` threw Next's raw 500 on non-multipart requests and queried a phantom `users` collection via the public client — now content-type-guarded (clean 400 "Request must be multipart/form-data…"), `users`→`members`, `consuela_saved_locations` read wrapped in a `[]` fallback, all on `withAdmin`. `email/forward` got the same `users`→`members` + `withAdmin` fix (now 200 end-to-end). **(4) Removed dead/dangerous routes:** legacy `/api/family` route + `family.json` (zero callers; leaked plaintext PINs on GET and allowed unauthenticated arbitrary file writes on POST) and the dead `/api/consuela/chat` stub + its integration-test block (unauthenticated PB, phantom `conversations`/`conversation_messages`, hardcoded TODO reply — real chat uses `/api/hermes/chat`). **(5) chat/process hardened:** `/api/chat/process` (enhanced-AI clarification layer `UnifiedInput` calls before `/api/hermes/chat`) 500'd on phantom `consuela_family_members`/`consuela_saved_locations`; those reads now fall back to `[]` and the ambiguity check is skipped when no family context loads, so it passes through cleanly (200, no clarification spam) and chat still flows to Hermes. **Known limitations (graceful degradation, not crashes):** money-mountain + time-capsule collections were never seeded and their schemas use `relation` fields to a nonexistent `users` collection — both degrade to empty 200s (data doesn't persist); the enhanced-AI family (`family-ai`/`consuela-ai-enhanced` context enrichment) is a parallel demo built on phantom collections and only its crash paths were fixed. Verified live (adult session): all GET routes 200 or deliberate 404 on removed routes; gateway reads 200; skill-tree/schedule-analytics/recurring-patterns/family-memory return real data; family-memory create/read/delete round-trips; removed routes 404; formData routes clean 400. Suite 555/555 (removed 2 consuela/chat tests, updated pb-rules-lockdown mock for autodate), `tsc` clean, eslint clean on all touched files.

- 2026-08-26 — fix(integrations): Settings → Integrations backend repaired — live PB reseeded, seed script rewired to the canonical seeder, graceful degradation on config-store/HA failures. **Root cause:** the live PocketBase (recreated 2026-08-21) predated HA Phase 2 + Services & Keys and was missing 14 collections (`consuela_service_config`, `ha_notify_config`, `ha_notify_prefs`, `ha_mirror_state`, `ha_entities/areas/devices/automations`, `skill_tree_profiles`, `skill_branches`, `quests`, `achievements`, `user_achievements`, `recurring_patterns`), so every `/api/services/*` route threw → bare 500s (🔑 card dead, Test buttons dead, runtime 500 → weather hardcoded coords) and the 🔔 card warned "config store unreachable". Compounding: `npm run pb:seed` ran a stale standalone `scripts/pb-seed.mjs` (original 10 collections only, no LOCKED_RULES) while the complete `seedCollections()` in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` was wired to nothing. **(1)** `scripts/pb-seed.mjs` rewritten as a thin wrapper: auto-loads `.env.local` via `process.loadEnvFile` when NEXT_PUBLIC_PB_URL/PB_ADMIN_EMAIL/PB_ADMIN_PASS aren't exported (explicit env still wins for the `scripts/consuela/test-*.mjs` flow), validates, then dynamic-imports `seedCollections()` AFTER env resolution (pb.ts/pb-auth.ts capture env at module load). **(2)** Seed run against the live instance: 14 collections created; `events` patched +importanceScore/importanceReason/importanceUpdatedAt; `recipes` +sourceUrl; drifted rules re-locked admin-only on members/meal_plan_entries/events/tasks/emergency_contacts/schedules/rewards/penalties. **(3)** Hardening: `GET /api/services/config` try/catch → 503 `{services:[], error:"config_store_unreachable"}` (was empty-body 500); `ServicesKeysCard` gained a `loadFailed` one-line error state; `GET /api/services/runtime` try/catch → 200 `{runtime:{}, error:...}` (widgets keep defaults); `GET /api/ha/health` try/catch → 200 `{ok:false, configured:false, reason:"not_configured", wsStatus:"disconnected", wsConnected:false}` (was 500 when HA_HOST/HA_TOKEN absent). Verified live (adult session): config GET 200 all 11 services, PUT/DELETE round-trip with source=db, Test buttons honest (themealdb ok 434ms; hermes unreachable; telegram bot ok but chat-id unset; gmail/HA not_configured), notify-targets warning gone, notify-test honest 400, Google state/device-grant untouched. Tests +3 (config 503 path, runtime empty path, ha-health not_configured). Suite 557/557, tsc clean, 0 new lint (1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect in ServicesKeysCard legacy-blob effect, present on HEAD).

Routes: /api/services/config GET(masks)/PUT/DELETE (adult gate), /test per-service health fns, /import (legacy blob), /runtime, /home-assistant/reconnect. Settings 🔑 card w/ status dots, masked fields, Test/Clear/Reconnect, import banner. - **Last Updated:** 2026-09-22 | **Kid mode: crew & open board** — NEW `src/modes/kid/kid-board.ts` (pure helpers: `kidBoardState` / `splitKidBoard` / `crewAvatars` / `crewProgressLabel` / `crewWaitingNames`, crew math imported from `@/lib/task-utils`) + NEW `src/modes/kid/KidCrewBoard.tsx` (presentation only; `data-testid="kid-crew-board"`, null when both lists empty; actionable rows are buttons labelled `Join crew:` / `Done my part:` / `Grab it:`). KidHome renders the board above "🎯 Your Quests"; `pendingTasks` narrowed to assigned-only (crew/open live on the board — XOR, never both); subtitle counts pending + board; 3-way empty state; `onAct={openQuestPin}` so join/check-in/grab reuse `/api/tasks/claim` (zero backend changes). - **Last Updated:** 2026-09-20 | **Tasks-screen UI lift — both modes (evaluation→plan→4 phases, all shipped as `tasks-lift-v1`).** A critique scored the surface 24/40 (dual-agent was denied by session rules → single-context run, detector 1 finding = the documented --ease-spring false positive). Evidence: on a 390px phone the chore list started at 1223px (guest) / 1750px (parent) behind stacked stat tiles + an EMPTY 393px AI card; the Tasks⇄Leaderboard segmented control was 32px (under the app's own 44px floor); member-tile names were 10.88px (under the 11px floor); PIN inputs had placeholder-only labels; 10 duplicate popBounce <style> blocks polluted the strip's textContent; kids could swipe-to-edit + DELETE family chores ungated with NO confirm; the kitchen display (guest after the 30-min auto-logout) could never complete a task. **P0 safety:** Add/Edit/Delete are parent-only (Delete now confirms via the shared Modal — "goes away for the whole family"); kids/pets hitting /tasks redirect to their KidHome quest surface (every entry path); the dead "Sync Google Tasks" button + RemindersSection (Google device-flow rejects the scope) are removed. **P1 hierarchy:** Pending leads the page (Open board directly above); stat tiles are one compact 3-up row at every width; "Consuela suggests" is parent-only and BELOW Completed (quiet "✨ Get chore ideas" line when idle); the Leaderboard tab leads with Podium + PrizeRaceCard, with Journey/FamilyGoal/HallOfFame folded behind a "🏅 Trophies, journey & history" expander; segmented options min-h-44; tile names .7rem; PIN inputs aria-labeled; AnimatedEmoji's 11 keyframes hoisted to globals.css (zero per-instance <style>). **P2 kid language + sessions:** NEW `src/modes/kid/quest-labels.ts` (pure, tested) — due dates render as "Today"/"Tomorrow"/weekday/"⚠️ Late" chips (accent-toned) instead of raw ISO; open quests say "🫳 Up for grabs" (not another kid's name), crew quests "🤝 Crew"; quests lead KidHome above the weekend banner; "🔄 Switch member" is one-tap on ALL kid surfaces (bedtime hides it); kid/pet sessions auto-logout at 15 idle min (parents 30) with a "⏳ N min left — tap anything to stay" chip in the final 5; /rewards routes to "/" when the kid session ends. **P3 parent efficiency:** "✓ Approve all (N)" pays the whole queue with ONE parent-PIN (same idempotent reversal-aware approve per row); Add-modal Points is a Stepper (0-50, aria-labeled) and Recurring is a select (None/Daily/Weekdays/Weekly). React-compiler bail fixed (mutable loop → reduce; openBoard/pendingApprovals memos → plain computations). Verified live on the NAS (390px): guest Pending at 562px (was 1223), 0 tiny text, 0 sub-44px targets, 0 <style> tags, docH 1790→1108; kid on /tasks lands on "/" in kid mode; KidHome quest chips render "🫳 Up for grabs · ⚠️ Late" (no raw dates), Switch member present, quests above the banner. Gates: tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2348/2351 (3 stash-proven pre-existing: TZ-streak ×2 + weather-strip), next build clean. Contracts in the 2026-09-20 UI Change Record.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-20 | **Ask Consuela: the thread stays on screen while Consuela is thinking, and replies survive tab changes (zero backend changes).** Two reported bugs, one architecture fix. (1) **History vanished during a reply** — `showHero` treated "first user message + isTyping" as a hero state, so the whole thread (the user's own bubble included) was replaced by a 200px "Thinking…" orb until the first content token, which with buffered tool rounds could be minutes. Now `showHero = !hasRealMessages`: the hero owns only a truly empty conversation, and the existing typing-dots bubble (with the tool `statusLine`) renders under the last message as the thinking affordance. The big hero orb branch is deleted. (2) **"If I change pages then it shows"** — chat state was component-local, and every tab navigation unmounts /chat, so an in-flight stream's updates landed on a dead component; the reply only appeared on remount because the page refetched today's PocketBase thread. NEW **`src/lib/chat-store.ts`** (module-level observable singleton, consumed with `useSyncExternalStore` — same idiom as `calendar-member-snapshot.ts`) now owns `{ messages, isTyping, statusLine, hydrated, streaming }` plus the whole stream (AbortController, msgCounter, PB watermark, hydration). Navigating away interrupts nothing; returning re-attaches to the live partial instantly. `ensureHydrated()` runs once per page load (localStorage merge + full PB day read) and later mounts do a cheap incremental `since` reconcile; localStorage persistence moved into the store (a reply landing while away is saved). The page keeps only UI-local state (speaker, draft, scroll pin, read-aloud, modals). NEW `tests/unit/chat-store.test.ts` (7) + 2 page regressions ("keeps the user's message and the thinking bubble visible while Consuela replies", "keeps streaming while the page is unmounted and shows the partial on return"); all four chat page suites call `__resetChatStoreForTests()` in `beforeEach`. Verified: chat suites 58/58, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2362 passed (323 files; the 2 uncaught teardown exceptions in `tasks-approve-all` are pre-existing and reproduce in isolation); `next build` clean. **Ops: none** (no PB schema/route changes). Contracts in the 2026-09-20 UI Change Record.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-20 | **Recipe detail page + cook mode shipped — saved recipes are now viewable and cookable (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-19-recipe-detail-page-cook-mode-design.md`, zero backend changes).** New route `/meals/recipes/[id]` renders the two-column sticky layout (hero + favorite, stats/macros, actions, quantity-styled ingredients left; numbered steps + source right) from the existing `useRecipes` catalog; if the recipe was deleted the page falls back to the meal's `meal_plan_entries` snapshot with an honest banner (edit/delete/favorite hidden) — the banner and not-found state are gated on a catalog-settled signal (recipes non-empty / syncBlocked / 600ms timer), so a slow catalog read never shows a false "deleted" or "not found". Pure helpers `src/lib/ingredient-quantity.ts` (`parseIngredientLine` — leading quantity/unit in amber bold, raw string never lost) and `src/lib/recipe-steps.ts` (`parseInstructionsToSteps` — newline-first, sentence-split fallback, marker stripping) power the page AND the new **CookMode** full-screen checklist (`src/components/meals/CookMode.tsx`: gather-ingredients + steps checkboxes, progress bar, same-day `cookmode:<id>:<YYYY-MM-DD>` localStorage persistence, auto-clear + 🎉 card when done, Esc exits, "No steps to check"/"Nothing to gather" counters on empty recipes). Entry points: RecipeBox card tap (`?from=recipes`; the view/keyboard affordance wraps only the image+name+stats region so inner action buttons are never ARIA-flattened) and the previously dead PlanTab meal-card chevron (`?from=plan`, `.hit-44`, hidden for meals without `recipeId`). Actions on the page: add-to-day picker, 🛒 to grocery, ✏️ edit (existing RecipeModal), 🗑 delete with confirm (finally renders the dead `deleteCatalogRecipe` prop), favorite. Verified: TDD red→green suites (ingredient-quantity 8, recipe-steps 7, cook-mode 6, recipe-detail-page 8, recipe-box-navigation 6, plan-tab-recipe-chevron 2), warm-glass contracts pass, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean, full suite 2325 passed / 2 failed (both documented pre-existing `calculate-real-streak-tz`). **Ops: none** (no PB schema/migration).
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-20 | **Points-display wave 2: guest completions + week champions — the kitchen display could never complete a task, and the Hall of Fame was silently empty.** User report: "this week points still not showing." Two live-verified root causes: **(1) Guest devices got "Wrong PIN" on a CORRECT PIN.** The kitchen display auto-logs-out after 30 min, so it is usually a GUEST session — and the middleware session gate 401'd `/api/members/verify` + `/api/tasks/claim` before any PIN check (verified: 401 with correct creds). Even past that gate, assigned-task completions were client-only writes a guest can never push (the snapshot POST 401s) — completions lived in one browser's localStorage forever. Fix: both routes are now middleware-exempt (they self-authenticate — the member PIN is the credential, server-verified + throttled, the same trust model as the already-exempt `/api/auth/login`; siblings stay gated, pinned by `middleware-exempt` tests) + NEW server-authoritative `complete`/`undo` actions on `/api/tasks/claim` (week-history-idempotent, reversal-aware; child completions land `pendingApproval` — kids never move points server-side; kid-pending undo reopens with the durable `sentBackAt` proof). The Tasks page's assigned-completion + undo flows persist through them (optimistic local update kept for offline tolerance; rollback ONLY on a definitive 409/400). **(2) `hall_of_fame.emoji` had PB's default 5000-char cap — every family member has a photo avatar (100-250KB base64), so EVERY enshrinement create failed `validation_max_text_constraint` silently.** This is why the Hall of Fame had 0 rows despite Aurora winning 2026-09-07 with 13 pts. Fix: pb-seed declares `emoji` `options.max 400000` (the members.emoji precedent) — the field-option drift pass healed the live field (`hall_of_fame (patched +1 field options (emoji))`); NEW `src/lib/hall-of-fame-backfill.ts` recomputes the enshrinement from server truth (week_archive + members + weekly_prizes — competition-ranked, ties share a rank, prize text frozen per rank, idempotent per member+weekStart, never touches existing rows so `celebrated:true` can't be lost) and runs self-healing inside `GET /api/tasks/sync` (the 60s refresh every signed-in device already performs); entries older than the newest archived week are created pre-celebrated (history, not stale ceremonies). **The weekly structure the family asked for is confirmed live:** weeks run Monday 00:00 → Sunday 23:59 (America/Detroit), the prize race locks the top 3 at Monday's rollover, weekly points reset while all-time is preserved (getMemberAllTimePoints = archive + current), and champions are now recorded automatically. Verified live: a GUEST (sessionless) `complete` with a correct PIN returned 200 and landed in BOTH week_data and the snapshot ({"Rebecca (Mom)":7} + the earn tx); the server `undo` reversed it (adjust −7, points net 0); one sync GET enshrined 2026-09-07 (Aurora 🥇 13 pts, Bailey/Emily/Jasmine 🥈 tied 10 pts, prize text frozen) AND recovered the archived 2026-06-15 week (pre-celebrated). Aurora + the kids' uncelebrated wins will fire their ceremonies on next open. Gates: tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2313 passed / 9 failed (all stash-proven pre-existing on HEAD: calculate-real-streak-tz ×2, use-meals-sync-blocked ×3, weather-widget ×4 load-flaky), `next build` clean. Deployed as `hall-heal-v1`. **Ops: none remaining** (pb:seed already healed the live emoji cap).

a new **🫳 Open** board; the "Up for grabs" filter renamed **"Open"** (now includes crew tasks with space); crew rows show join/check-in progress; crew-aware "Needs approval" rows read "🤝 Crew N/M · X pts each"; a parent-only **Crew tasks** management card with per-member remove (PIN-gated). **KidHome:** crew tasks join/check-in through the same server actions (under-10 PIN-free), crew-aware pending list. **AI suggest:** `mapTaskIdeas` + the planner validator carry `mode`/`crewSize`/`speedBonus` (crew size capped to the non-pet roster; open/crew rows need no assignee; assigned rows still drop unknown assignees). (2) **New conversation never cleared the view** — `/new` appended a divider but the render mapped the whole `messages` array. Fix: NEW pure `src/lib/chat-thread.ts` (`threadKey` dedupes on role+content — drops `speaker`, killing the signed-out duplicate row; `sortThread` orders by a new `Message.at` epoch-ms key with user→divider→reply ties; additive `mergeThread` never removes rows; `visibleThread` slices at the newest `system` marker and drops the seed greeting once a user row exists). Both hydration effects (PB + localStorage) now MERGE into state instead of replacing; the page renders `visibleMessages = visibleThread(sortThread(messages))` for the thread map + hero/quick-actions/read-aloud; `showHero` now hands the view to the thread once any real message exists (not only a user row); the seed greeting is never persisted; `selectChatMessages` sorts `"createdAt,id"`; the confirm copy is honest that the screen clears while PB keeps history. Verified: NEW `tests/unit/chat-thread.test.ts` (18) + a delayed-hydration ordering page test; chat suites 42/42; full suite **2228 passed / 3 failed — all 3 stash-proven pre-existing on HEAD** (calculate-real-streak-tz ×2 + weather-widget strip ×1); tsc clean; eslint 0 on touched files. - **Last Updated:** 2026-09-17 | **Backend security/race fix wave — 8 scan findings fixed TDD red→green (PIN throttle, kid write-tool gates, ledger/alert-state/snapshot races, Hall of Fame dupes).** (1) **PIN brute-force throttle** in `src/lib/server-auth.ts`: in-memory per-(source,name) lockout — 5 consecutive wrong PINs → 30s lock, a rejected attempt DURING the lock extends it another 30s, a success resets; wired into BOTH `verifyPinFromPB` + `verifyPinAgainstAnyMember` (login, emergency, alarm, admin-pin, claim, redeem all inherit it; restart clears state — LAN-appropriate). (2) **Kid write-tool gates:** `/api/consuela/suggestions/act` now role-gates its tool dispatch — a verified child/pet PIN gets 403 `adult_only` on the 6 write tools (`add_grocery_item`, `add_task`, `complete_task`, `complete_grocery_item`, `add_event`, `remove_event`); `get_grocery_list` + `dismiss_suggestion` stay any-member; and the **Clem branch** of `/api/hermes/chat` now passes the session-derived `role` into `buildToolsForOpenAI` — child/pet/guest Clem sessions arm only the 4 read grocery tools (previously the CLEM filter bypassed the kid allowlist and armed both grocery writes for anyone, violating §5.3's "kids get read-only" contract). (3) **Week-ledger races:** NEW `src/lib/keyed-lock.ts` (generic in-process keyed mutex; FIFO, error-swallowing tails) + `src/lib/week-ledger-lock.ts` (names the week_data domain); `/api/tasks/claim` + `/api/rewards/redeem` wrap their ENTIRE withAdmin flow in the lock — orchestrated-race tests red-proved the old code told BOTH concurrent claimants "success" while only one earn persisted, and a claim vs redeem could silently erase the other's tx; now the second writer re-reads fresh and loses honestly (409 already-claimed/already_completed) or both legit txs persist. (4) **ha_alert_state races:** both `*/15` alert crons serialize load→decide→push→save under the keyed lock and save via NEW `src/lib/ha/alert-state.ts` CAS (expected-prev refusal, the `setState` idiom) — two overlapping runs used to BOTH push the same storm/event alert; now exactly one push, and a stale save can never clobber the winner's alerted stamp. (5) **Hall of Fame dupes:** pb-seed declares UNIQUE `idx_hall_of_fame_member_week (member, weekStart)` + a pre-index `dedupeHallOfFameRows` heal (keeps the celebrated row, else earliest) so the index can build over legacy dupes; `db.insertHallOfFameEntry` became an upsert by (member, weekStart) that never loses a `celebrated:true`. (6) **Snapshot merge:** `mergeTasksSnapshot` now also dedupes WITHIN the snapshot (by id AND title) — a blob carrying the same logical row twice used to land both (duplicate chores + colliding React keys). (7) **tasks/sync parent legs:** the non-parent tasks-leg path re-read stored parent-owned legs and wrote them back; interleaved with a parent's full push it RESURRECTED the parent's just-replaced points/rewards — the whole read-modify-write now runs under the keyed lock and re-reads fresh. **Ops on deploy: `npm run pb:seed`** (creates the hall_of_fame UNIQUE index + dedupes legacy duplicate wins). Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean; eslint 0 on all touched files; full suite **2198 passed / 3 failed — all 3 proven pre-existing on HEAD via git-stash** (TZ-streak ×2 + weather-strip ×1); every fix red-proved first (new suites: `pin-throttle` 6, `suggestion-act-role-gate` 9, clem role-gate +3, `week-ledger-lock` 3, `ha-alert-state` 4, alert-route races ×2, `hall-of-fame-dedupe` 8, snapshot-merge +2, `tasks-sync-race` 1).
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-18 | **MUSE admin toggle is now LIVE — flipping "Allow admin operations" takes effect immediately for already-connected agents (no re-login).** User report: an external agent connected with a MUSE key said it was on a restricted ("kids") surface; admin had been switched on but the agent still saw the 47-tool non-admin catalog. **Root cause:** admin was the intersection of the token's mint-time `adm` claim and the live `adminEnabled` row toggle (`authorizeMuseRequest`: `adm = result.adm && row.adminEnabled`), and login signs `adm` from `row.adminEnabled` — so an agent that logged in *before* the toggle was flipped kept `adm:false` forever, and revoking/rotating was required to upgrade it. **Fix (TDD red→green):** `src/lib/muse/auth.ts` now returns `adm = row.adminEnabled` (the token claim is a diagnostics-only snapshot) — turning admin on grants the 5 admin tools to every connected agent on its next `GET /api/muse/tools`, turning it off revokes them instantly. Updated `src/app/api/muse/tools/route.ts` + `src/lib/muse/execute.ts` + `src/lib/muse/token.ts` comments, `docs/muse-api.md` (catalog note + the "Admin tools require the live settings toggle" invariant), and AGENTS.md §2.3/§5.6. Tests: `muse-authorize` case table re-pinned to live semantics; `muse-tools-route` +2 cross-cases (non-admin token + toggle on ⇒ admin tools present; admin-claim token + toggle off ⇒ absent). No schema/ops change. Verified: `muse-*` suites 65/65 (+2), full suite green, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-16 | **Chat "ran out of steps" fixed — forced wrap-up final round (MAX_ROUNDS 4→6) + AI Health observability + Settings AI Models is now a real manager (view chain, reorder, test, confirm-delete).** User report: "/new conversations keep failing with 'I kept needing to look things up and ran out of steps', and I can't tell if that's a timeout or step exhaustion — and Settings can't tell me what LLM is actually set up." Three root causes found and fixed: **(1) Tool-loop starvation on fresh contexts.** The first message after `/new` sends an empty history, so broad questions (the hero chips) push the model into multi-tool read chains that burned the old `MAX_ROUNDS=4` before it ever wrote a prose answer. Fix in `src/app/api/hermes/chat/route.ts` (both streamed + buffered paths): `MAX_ROUNDS` 4→6 and the FINAL round is a **forced tool-free wrap-up** — no `tools`/`tool_choice` offered, an appended system note ("…answer now using what you already gathered — no more lookups"), so the model must render content from what it has. The exhaustion message is now the rare last resort (only when even the wrap-up round fails on every target), and `callAiStream` drops both `tools`+`tool_choice` keys when no tools are armed (mirrors `callAi`; avoids provider 400s on contradicting keys). **(2) Zero admin observability.** New `src/lib/ai/health.ts` — an in-memory 50-entry ring buffer of chat outcomes (`ok`/`wrapup`/`exhausted`/`snag`/`client_gone`/`unconfigured` + rounds/ms/brain/targets/reason; METADATA ONLY — never message content) recorded by every chat request exit path, served by new `GET /api/ai/health` (session-gated, same gate as `/api/ai/providers`; resets on container restart — docker logs are the durable record). Per-target warnings now log elapsed ms (`[ai] stream target X failed: TimeoutError (60123ms)`) so docker logs also distinguish timeouts from HTTP errors. **(3) Settings could only ADD models, never SEE them.** `GET /api/ai/providers` now computes `active` from `resolveChatTargets()` (the same resolver chat uses) instead of the PB table alone — the old PB-only derivation rendered "No brain configured" while chat answered fine on the legacy env fallback; when the resolved chain is env/legacy, the route also returns `envProviders` (read-only pseudo-provider groups with `{provider, baseUrl, models, keyPreview, readOnly:true}`) so the card shows what's actually answering. `AiModelsCard.tsx` rewritten: provider rows now list every model as a chip tagged **Brain**/**Fallback** with ↑/↓ arrows that persist the order via PUT (first model answers), provider rows get ↑/↓ (swaps `order` fields via two PUTs — provider 1's first model is the brain) plus a ⚡ **Test** button (POST /api/ai/models, shows "ok — N models · Xms" / error line), 🗑️ now opens the shared portaled confirm Modal (was instant-delete with no confirm), and a new **AI Health** section shows the outcome summary ("Last 20: 14 ok · 2 wrap-up · 1 timeout · avg 3.4s") + last-failure line + outcome dot trail with a manual Refresh. Verified: full suite 2148/2148 (295 files), tsc clean (excluding the pre-existing hall-of-fame WIP file), eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean. No new PB collections → no `npm run pb:seed` needed.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-16 | **Home leaderboard points never live-refreshed + two streak bugs (TDD red→green).** User report: "members are not displaying the points they are earning in the tasks they are making." Root cause: `useLeaderboardData` (`src/components/leaderboard/hooks/useLeaderboardData.ts`) read the week/tasks stores ONCE at mount (`useEffect(..., [])`) with NO listener for the 60s `consuela-data-refreshed` pulse — the only Home data source missing it (sibling `useWeeklyPrizes` in the same widget has one; `db/index.ts` documents the "Home's widgets re-read on `consuela-data-refreshed`" contract). A Home view that stayed mounted (kitchen display / PWA — the normal case) froze at its mount-time points: completions on /tasks or another device landed in the store via `applyTasksSnapshotToStores` but the widget never re-read, so earned points stayed invisible until a manual reload. It now bumps a version on `consuela-data-refreshed` + `consuela-members-updated` and re-reads (the same idiom KidHome and the Tasks page use); a full navigate-away-and-back already remounted and re-read, so only stayed-mounted views were affected. Two further bugs surfaced while testing: (2) the hook passed the WHOLE FAMILY's completion dates to `calculateRealStreak`, breaking its documented "must already be filtered to the member" contract — every member showed the same aggregate streak; now filtered per member via `getThisWeeksCompletedDates(tasks, name)` as the Tasks page already did. (3) `calculateRealStreak` (`src/lib/task-utils.ts`) compared a UTC-midnight cursor against a Monday derived from LOCAL midnight (`mondayOf(...).toISOString()`), so on any zone BEHIND UTC — the NAS and every family phone run `TZ=America/Detroit` — the Monday cursor (00:00Z) sorted before Monday (04:00Z) and the walk exited one day early, undercounting streaks; the loop is now pure date-string comparison (lexical, offset-immune) and byte-identical under UTC. Verified TDD: NEW `tests/unit/calculate-real-streak-tz.test.ts` is pinned to `America/Detroit` and was confirmed RED against the pre-fix code (expected 3, got 2 — Monday dropped) via a temporary revert; +3 refresh/per-member-streak tests in `use-leaderboard-data.test.tsx` (also TZ-pinned now, mirroring `task-utils.test.ts`). Full suite 2098/2098 (291 files), tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files. Ops: none — any open Home view recovers its points on the next 60s sync cycle.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-15 | **Weekly Prize Race shipped — parents set up to 3 prizes in Settings, kids race all week, Monday's rollover locks the top 3 and each winner sees their prize exactly once (across all devices).** New PB collection `weekly_prizes` (`rank` required + UNIQUE `idx_weekly_prizes_rank`, parent-write gateway policy) + `seedWeeklyPrizes` create-if-absent seeding on `npm run pb:seed`, localStorage mirror `consuela-weekly-prizes` with an LWW stamp (`consuela-weekly-prizes-stamp`) riding the tasks snapshot leg; `hall_of_fame` gained optional `prize` (text frozen at rollover — later prize edits never rewrite history) + `celebrated` (single-fire) fields. Week rollover (`archiveWeekWinner`) now enshrines the TOP 3 by competition rank with their frozen prize text — idempotent per member+weekStart, keeps the latest 12 WEEKS (≈36 rows), and a zero-point week records NO entries. Settings gains the parent-only 🏆 **Weekly prizes** card (add/remove rows, ranks stay contiguous, footer "Winners are locked in when the week resets Monday."). The Tasks Leaderboard gains the **PrizeRaceCard** above the podium (one row per configured prize with its current holder — ties share a rank, empty ranks say "Up for grabs" — the ⏳ "Resets in N days" countdown, and a personal aria-live gap line; with fewer than 3 prizes the gap chases the LAST defined rank, e.g. #2 when only two are set). Podium slots wear a 🎁 prize ribbon (only when the slot has points — a fresh week's "crown is up for grabs" state owns zero-point weeks), and **week-vs-all-time labeling** landed everywhere: podium slots show a quiet "{N} all-time" line, YourCard appends "· {N} all-time", the Earned StatTile reads "Earned this week" with an "{N} pts all-time" detail (follows the member filter), and KidHome's hero carries the all-time caption keyed by the ledger-resolved full name. The Monday **WeeklyWinModal** ceremony mounts on Home (suppressed on the wall profile), Tasks, and KidHome: a winner with an un-celebrated prized top-3 entry gets confetti + "You won: {prize}"; every close path claims it (local flag first, then a best-effort POST to the new **`/api/hall-of-fame/celebrate`** — session-gated; child/pet sessions may claim ONLY their own win while parents may claim for anyone, because gateway hall_of_fame writes are parent-only; no prized win → 404; already-claimed → idempotent 200), so the celebration fires exactly once per winner across the family's devices. The previously dead **Weekly Champ 🥇** badge is now earned from any rank-1 hall entry. Home's leaderboard widget gained a one-line race footer (prize text clipped at 30 chars, `line-clamp-1`: signed-out "🥇 🥈 🥉 prizes this week", holding "— you're holding it!", off-podium points gap, fresh week "New week — prizes up for grabs") and KidHome a positive-only race line ("🎉 You're winning {prize}!" / "{N} more points to win {prize}!" / "Earn points to win this week's prize!"). All race math stays pure in `task-utils` (`loadWeeklyPrizes`/`saveWeeklyPrizes`/`prizeForRank`/`raceGap`/`getGapMessage`/`uncelebratedWinFor`/`markWinCelebrated` + stamp trio) and every surface re-reads on the 60s `consuela-data-refreshed` pulse. **Ops on deploy: `npm run pb:seed`** (adds the rank UNIQUE index to the live instance — verified idempotent: first run `weekly_prizes (patched +1 indexes: idx_weekly_prizes_rank)`, second `weekly_prizes (already exists)`). Verified: full suite **2095/2095 (290 files)**, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean. Spec/plan `docs/superpowers/specs|plans/2026-09-15-weekly-prize-race*` (outer repo). Contracts in the 2026-09-15 UI Change Record. **Final-review fix wave (2026-09-16, commit 7c5e896):** the "exactly once across devices" ceremony claim is now true in production — `syncHallOfFameToPB` carries `prize`/`celebrated` into PB (was stripped), and a new `loadHallOfFameMerged()` downlink (local content wins; PB `celebrated: true` always wins; merged + persisted) drives `useLeaderboardData` + `WeeklyWinModal`, converging on the 60s pulse (the gateway `updateHallOfFameEntry` write stays parent-policy; kids claim through the `/api/hall-of-fame/celebrate` route). `MemberSheet` gains the `hasWeeklyChamp` seam so a winner's 🥇 reads EARNED in their own profile sheet (was: locked while the leaderboard showed it). The Hall of Fame now lists weekly rows (latest first) with the frozen prize text visible. `PrizeRaceCard` gained the zero-week "New race started — prizes reset Monday to Monday" line + holder avatars; its record-when-changed sync POST includes `weeklyPrizes`; `WeeklyPrizesCard` no longer clobbers in-progress parent edits on the refresh pulse (dirty guard); `/api/tasks/sync` `ignoredLegs` honestly lists the `weeklyPrizes` legs for non-parent posts. Verified: full suite **2148/2148 (295 files)**, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-15 | **PocketBase routing audit closed (all 8 findings) + MUSE inbound API identity shipped.** **Part A — routing cleanup (Home-ai):** (F1) the emergency path read a process-start contact cache — new `liveEmergencyContacts()` reads PB live and both `/api/emergency` + `GET /api/emergency-contacts` report `contactsSource: "live"|"cache"` (cache only as an honest fallback); (F2) `/api/db/*` is now **per-collection write-gated** (`WRITE_POLICY`/`canWrite` — `week_data`/`week_archive`/`rewards`/`penalties`/`hall_of_fame`/`family_goals`/`emergency_contacts`/`events`/`schedules`/`meal_plan_entries`/`recipes`/`meal_week_archive`/`chat_messages`/`morning_briefing`/`proactive_suggestions`/`consuela_state` = **parent-only** → 403 `adult_only`; `tasks`/`grocery_list_items`/`pantry_items` = any signed-in session) and `POST /api/tasks/sync` for a non-parent syncs the tasks leg only (`ignoredLegs: ["weekData","rewards","penalties"]`); the follow-on regression — kid reward redemptions were writing `week_data` through that now-parent-only gateway — is fixed by the new **server-authoritative `POST /api/rewards/redeem`** (server reads the cost, validates the balance, appends the redeem tx; 60s dedupe → 409, unknown reward → 404, insufficient → 400, wrong/missing PIN → 401); (F7) `authorizeAdminRequest` is a **parent allowlist** (child/pet → 403 `adult_only`); (F3/F4) `/api/consuela/briefing` requires a session (PATCH stamps `acknowledgedBy`) and the mutating HA routes (`call-service`/`notify-config`/`notify-prefs`/`notify-test`) require a parent session — the stale "unauthenticated by design" comments in `ha/sync` + `ha/notify-targets` corrected to "session-level"; (F5) the dead browser-direct `auth_sessions` path is gone from `useAuth`/`pb-db.ts` (localStorage is the device-session store; grep-guard pinned); (F6) `/api/recurring-patterns` uses `getAuthedPB()`; (F8) money-mountain/skill-tree/time-capsules identity is **session-derived** (the client `x-user-id` is ignored), `/api/recipes/ingest` requires a session (only `/api/recipes/search` stays public), the week-rollover archive now writes an idempotent `week_archive` row per `weekStart` (so `get_past_weeks` is live), the 3 dead HA seed collections were dropped from `pb-seed.ts`, `/api/google/webhook` was deleted (the 5-min cron is the sync path), and the gateway `sort` param is whitelisted (400 `invalid_sort`). **Part B — MUSE inbound API identity (`/api/muse/*`, LAN/Tailscale-only, middleware-exempt but self-gated):** an operator generates a key in **Settings → MUSE** (SHA-256 hash stored; plaintext shown exactly once), an agent exchanges it at `POST /api/muse/auth/login` for a **24-hour HMAC bearer token** (distinct `muse-token-v1:` context; rotate/revoke bump the identity version and kill live tokens instantly), then uses `GET /api/muse/tools` (the **full adult 47-tool catalog; the 5 admin tools appear only when the admin toggle is on — off by default**), `POST /api/muse/tool` (allowlist-first dispatch via `executeMuseTool`; an admin tool on a non-admin token is 403 `tool not allowed`; redacted-args audit + per-key rate limit), `GET /api/muse/context?scope=meal|task|schedule|all` (grounded snapshot + prompt), and `GET /api/muse/docs` (public markdown reference). `/api/muse/settings*` + `/api/muse/log` are **adult-session gated** (never the bearer token). The **propose-only invariant holds**: MUSE can `propose_point_adjustment` but cannot apply it (a parent's PIN-confirmed chip executes it), and task approvals stay in the UI. **Ops on deploy: `npm run pb:seed`** (creates `consuela_muse` + `consuela_muse_log`, and carries the briefing `acknowledgedBy` field) before/at deploy. Verified: live probe `scripts/consuela/verify-muse-api.mjs` **12/12 ALL CHECKS PASSED** (parent-session settings envelope → generate a key **only when `hasKey:false`** → login `scopes:["tools"] admin:false` → whoami `admin:false` → 47-tool catalog with all 5 admin tools ABSENT → `get_weather` returns a result → `trigger_update` 403 → no-token 401 → re-rotate kills the old token → revoke+disable leaves MUSE inert; **no family-data writes**, never clobbers an existing operator key), tsc clean, eslint 0 new, full suite + `next build` green. Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/specs|plans/2026-09-15-pocketbase-routing-cleanup-and-muse-api*` (outer repo). Contracts in §2.3 + §5.6.

Also `add_meal` made weekOf-aware + one-time `scripts/consuela/heal-meal-weekof.mjs`; pb-seed `tasks` fields ride the wave. **Ops: run `node scripts/consuela/heal-meal-weekof.mjs` (dry-run) → `--apply`, plus `npm run pb:seed`, on deploy.** §5.3/§5.4/§5.5 rewritten to the shipped surface (they agree with `ai/TOOLS.md`, parity-guarded by `write-ai-boot.mjs`). - **Last Updated:** 2026-09-14 | **Tiered house-alert notifications — severe weather + important calendar events now push to phones/Telegram; routines surface in-app; per-category opt-in toggles (default OFF).** Two new `*/15` cron routes (`src/app/api/cron/consuela/weather-alert`, `calendar-alert`) each call a pure decision core in `src/lib/ha/alerts.ts` (`withinQuietHours` 9pm–7am America/Detroit, `weatherEpisodeDecision`, `calendarLeadDecisions`) and fan out via the existing `broadcastHouseAlert()`; a push is recorded as alerted only when `sent > 0` so a dead channel retries. Weather = current-hour storm (`code ≥ 95`) or heavy-snow (`73/75/85/86`) with episode-start dedupe until it clears; calendar = `importanceScore ≥ 50` event 0–60 min out, one push per `event+date`. In-app tier: `scanRoutinesDue` in `engine.ts` surfaces today's `schedules` routines starting ≤30 min as a `routine_due` suggestion (with `expiresAt` = start time, and `fetchExistingConditionKeys` now ignores expired pending rows so a routine re-fires the next day). Opt-in prefs generalize `ha_notify_prefs` to rows `key ∈ {briefing, weather, calendar}` (default OFF — finally gives the previously-dead briefing pref a write path), served by new `/api/ha/notify-prefs` GET/POST (create-on-404, mirrors `notify-config`) and a **"What to send"** block in `HaNotificationsCard`. New `ha_alert_state` collection (key+json) holds episode/event dedupe state across runs and restarts; severity WMO sets extracted to pure `src/lib/weather-severity.ts` (imported by both `WeatherSkins.ts` and the server, re-exported from skins so `WeatherWidget` imports are unchanged). Ops: `npm run pb:seed` (creates `ha_alert_state`, seeds the 3 default prefs, adds `routine_due` to the kind select) + add the two `*/15` lines to the host crontab. Tests +new suites: weather-severity, ha-alerts, ha-notify-prefs-route, ha-weather-alert-route, ha-calendar-alert-route, engine-routine-due, pb-seed-notify-prefs, + card prefs toggle.
