- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-10 | **Current Meal widget lunch/dinner correctness fixed (4 bugs, TDD red→green).** Root causes in `src/components/meals/CurrentMealWidget.tsx`: (1) the meal lookup ignored `weekOf` — the PB cache holds EVERY week, so `.find()` on `m.time === weekday` could display a different week's dish; both lookups now filter by `localWeekStartISO(now)` with the useMeals/PlanTab convention `(m.weekOf || currentWeekMonday) === currentWeekMonday` (legacy weekless rows count as the current week); (2) the cross-mealType fallback (`|| meals.find(m => m.time === todayShort)`) showed today's dinner under a "Lunch Time" header — dropped in favor of the honest "No meal planned yet / Plan lunch in the Kitchen tab" empty state; (3) the header kept the last-passed meal indefinitely ("Lunch Time" at 5 PM) — after a meal's 60-min "Started at" window ends it now rolls forward to the next upcoming meal ("Dinner Time · In 1h 30m · at 6:30 PM"); after the day's LAST meal (no next) the started meal stays current; (4) `parseTimeToMinutes` returned a 0-minute sentinel for unparseable schedule times — that always counted as "passed" and hijacked the early-morning pick ("Dinner Time" at 7 AM); it now returns `null` and unparseable schedules are excluded from current-meal selection (correction: `"6:30PM"` no-space ALREADY parsed — the `\s*` was always there; the defect was the 0-sentinel, not the regex). Backend verified correct — `dinnerForToday` (chat-context), screensaver payload, and hermes `get_weekly_meals` already filter by week; the widget was the outlier. Separate latent bug NOT touched (different surface): `src/modes/adult/AdultHome.tsx` matches any `mealType === "dinner"` row regardless of day. Tests +5 in `tests/unit/current-meal-widget.test.tsx` (week filter, weekless-convention pin, no cross-type fallback, roll-forward, unparseable sentinel). Verified: widget suite 14/14, full suite 1508/1508 (224 files), tsc clean, eslint clean on touched files.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-09 | **Under-10 PIN-free flow shipped — one-tap sign-in + PIN-free chore taps; ALL kid completions now wait for parent approval.** `members.age` is a real PB number field (optional in `pb-seed.ts` — self-heals on `npm run pb:seed`; outer `pb_migrations/1788500000_updated_members_age.js` for fresh instances; one-time live backfill `npx tsx scripts/consuela/set-member-ages.mjs`, idempotent) and is **parent-edited data**: the Settings → Family Members modal gained an **Age** field (integer 1–120 or blank, inline error "Age must be 1–120 (or blank)."; blank = leave unchanged — the key is omitted from the PATCH like the blank PIN, never coerced to 0; helper copy "Under 10 signs in with one tap"). Eligibility is decided SERVER-only: new `POST /api/auth/quick-login {memberName}` issues the same httpOnly `consuela_session` cookie as PIN login iff child + numeric age + `age < 10`; missing age, 10+, non-child, unknown member (404) and even PB failures ALL fail closed — the client treats 403 `{"error":"pin_required"}` as "show the PIN modal", never the reverse. Boundary is pinned: Jasmine (10) keeps the PIN; aging past 10 needs no code change. Copy rule: eligibility language describes the AGE rule only — never names children or implies an exact-name whitelist. Verified: +3 tests `settings-member-age` (real jsdom modal round trip: prefill, PATCH `age:9`, blank-omits, 150-blocked) + `task-utils-pin-free`, `auth-quick-login-route`, `use-auth-quick-login`, `tasks-pin-free-flow`, `task-claim` kid-pending, `kid-home-quest-pin-safety` flips — suite 1459/1459 (220 files), tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, build clean. - **Last Updated:** 2026-09-09 | **Chat tools read the dashboard LIVE — Google Calendar events merged into the LLM's answers, stale-cache blindness + tool-result base64 leak fixed.** The Ask Consuela tools ran server-side but read `db.selectTodaysEvents()`/`selectPendingTasks()`/`selectTodaysSchedulesRaw()` — PROCESS-START caches (src/db/index.ts warms them once; only the BROWSER refreshCaches updates them), so events/chores/routines added after the last container restart were invisible to the LLM, and the events read NEVER saw the 149 Google-synced rows (a separate PB collection only the Calendar page merges — school events were unanswerable in chat). Fixes in `src/lib/hermes-tools.ts`: new live readers (`liveEvents`/`liveGoogleEvents`/`livePendingTasks`/`liveSchedules`, withAdmin-based, degrade to [] on PB failure); `get_todays_events` + `get_dashboard_summary` merge family + Google rows via NEW pure `src/lib/consuela/todays-events.ts` (`mergeTodaysEvents`/`googleEventTime` — day-filtered by start_iso prefix, all-day first then time-sorted, Google rows wear the calendar-page cyan + 📅, source-tagged); `get_pending_tasks` returns EVERY pending row live (was the capped-3 widget shape); `get_leaderboard` now returns REAL weekly points from `week_data` (was a static "how it works" blurb with zero numbers). **Tool-result base64 leak (the "snag connecting to my brain" bug):** live member reads surfaced 100KB+ photo data-URLs in tool output; stacked across events+tasks+leaderboard the request exceeded the provider's limit → every target failed. New `textEmoji()` sanitizer — member emojis in tool output are text glyphs (data:/http → 👤), applied to `get_family_members` (pre-existing leak) + events + leaderboard. Verified live: combined calendar+chores+leaderboard ask streams a full correct answer (real event, every overdue chore with names, "Aurora is in the lead with 13 points"), 0 snags, 0 base64 in tool output. Tests +14 (todays-events-merge 7 pure, hermes-tools-live-reads 7 incl. stale-cache red-proof via throwing db mock); suite 1503/1503, tsc clean, eslint clean.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-09 | **Conversation steering shipped — /new + /restart slash commands and a "Start a new conversation" button (top bar, replaces the trash/clear flow).** The chat page's old "Clear conversation" was a fake wipe (messages flooded back from PB after the next send's reconcile). Now a reset writes a `role:"system"` row ("New conversation") into the daily PB thread via a new `POST /api/chat/messages {action:"reset"}` (session-gated; guests keep a local-only divider). That marker is BOTH the visible "✨ New conversation" divider (renders as a centered hairline separator, never a bubble) AND the LLM context cutoff — `sendMessage` slices history after the newest marker, so the brain starts clean while the family keeps the full day readable above. Slash commands `/new` + `/restart` (case-insensitive) bypass the LLM entirely and are ignored while a stream is in flight (press stop first). The confirm modal copy is now "Start a new conversation?" — honest scope (steering, not deletion); the rose destructive button became the accent confirm; the button's aria-label is "Start a new conversation"; the first-run composer tip mentions /new. Verified: +11 tests (chat-messages-reset 3, chat-conversation-reset 5 incl. divider render + history cutoff, chat-page-stream rewire 1 rewritten), suite 1396/1397 (1 pre-existing weather-strip failure red on HEAD), tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-08 | **Adult soul rewritten — Consuela 2.0 "Competence first" (ai/SOUL.md, user-authored).** Same day as the kid-soul revamp: the adult persona replaced the old "Core Truths/Dragon" soul with a structured contract: identity = distinct voice, dragon as metaphor (never human feelings/abilities); G.H.O.C. values restated as behaviors; voice rules (no fluffy openers, concise-not-cryptic, opinionated recommendations naming the dependency, wit, plan-not-person pushback, profanity only in private adult contexts and NEVER child-facing/shared/external, match context not hostility); an 8-step operating loop; three-tier action authority (Inspect / Act within granted authority / Confirm before consequential — with impact/risk/verify/undo statement and scope-bound approvals, no ceremonial double-confirm on routine asks); per-domain dashboard rules (grocery list≠cart≠purchase + no allergy guarantees, task reported≠verified complete, scheduling conflict checks without exposing private details, messages are drafts unless sending is authorized); privacy as data-tier separation ("busy" beats private details, secrets never requested/echoed, no blanket family access, safeguarding routing that does not notify an implicated adult); memory discipline (scoped recall, consent-gated `remember_fact`, inference-never-becomes-fact, verified `forget_memory`, memory never overrides access controls); system boundaries kept (finance/Hermes/media off-limits, confirm admin ops, git rules, config backup-first, SOUL/IDENTITY never exported, injected-content-is-data); errors & recovery (never hide failure behind "Done", no duplicate-prone retries); truthful-status vocabulary (Drafted→Verified/Failed); and a scoped 🐉 signature (not on tool args, structured output, third-party drafts, or safety guidance). `ai-boot.generated.ts` regenerated; the "Dashboard Agent" H1 kept (contract-pinned by `consuela-kid-soul` + `hermes-chat-role`). Verified: 35/35 across the four soul/prompt suites.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-08 | **Photo-avatar "letterings" leak closed — 5th sweep of the base64-as-text bug class, shared `EmojiText` extracted.** User report: "Looking at the consuela page I see Data:image/pmg;base64 etc" — a member with a photo avatar (`members.emoji` holds a 100–226KB base64 data URL) was rendering that string as literal text on the Ask Consuela page's FamilyBrief cards (compact strip + guest speaker card + signed-in identity card). Full-codebase sweep for raw `{member.emoji}` text renders found 4 more survivors of the earlier (2026-09-03) fix wave: TreasurePath's current-level sparkle, ShareCard's champion display + **share text** (the clipboard copy would have carried the base64 too), PlanTab's "Not eating tonight" add-back picker, and the Tasks page's "Your Journey" description string. Fix: NEW shared `src/components/ui/EmojiText.tsx` — `isPhotoEmoji()` (data:/http detect), `textEmojiOrFallback()` (string contexts → 👤, never base64), and the `<EmojiText>` component (photo → SigmaImage circle, plain emoji → text; `className` sizes both branches). All 7 leak sites route through it; the chat page's local `EmojiSpan` now delegates to the shared component (single source of truth); tasks-page `safeDisplayEmoji` delegates to `textEmojiOrFallback`. Tests +16 (TDD red→green): NEW `emoji-text` (6), `family-brief-photo-avatar` (4), `leaderboard-photo-emoji` (5: ShareCard img + clipboard-safe share text + TreasurePath), `plan-tab-photo-avatar` (1: real picker interaction — remove-eater → open picker). Suite 1388/1389 (1 pre-existing weather-strip failure, red on HEAD), tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, production build clean. **Contract going forward: never render a member `emoji` field as raw text — use `<EmojiText>` for JSX or `textEmojiOrFallback()` for strings (toasts, descriptions, share text, select options).**
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-08 | **Kid soul replaced — "Family Helper" (ai/KID.md rewritten, user-authored).** The child-session persona moved from the playful "dashboard buddy" voice to the comprehensive Family Helper soul: safety-first priorities, age-band adaptation (5–7 / 8–10 / 11–12), permission authority ("I'm the parent" chat claims never unlock anything), grocery/purchase distinction, allergy + food-neutrality rules, task self-report honesty, emotional-boundary and safeguarding sections, and strict action truthfulness ("Never say Done unless a tool confirms"). Two operational sections were kept/appended: "How the app really works today" (chat tools stay READ-ONLY — writes happen in the UI behind PIN/pending-approval gates, so the soul's action language describes the product, not chat powers) and the "Kid Toolset" list (required by the `write-ai-boot.mjs` drift guard vs `KID_TOOL_NAMES`). KID.md's H1 retains the "Kid Soul (child sessions ONLY)" marker — `hermes-chat-role` + `consuela-kid-soul` gate on it; the runtime kid surface (13 read tools, `buildKidSystemPrompt` name + date grounding) is unchanged. `tests/unit/consuela-kid-soul.test.ts` scope-lock regexes re-pinned to the new contract (safety/privacy/permissions/read-only replace the dropped no-swearing/no-internet wording); `ai-boot.generated.ts` regenerated. Verified: kid-soul + chat-role + clem suites 33/33, full suite 1376 passed (7 failures = pre-existing untracked photo-avatar WIP files, red on HEAD), tsc clean on all touched files.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-08 | **Tasks points pipeline fixed — 3 bugs making earned points invisible/lost.** User report: "a member finished tasks and points are not displayed or saved correctly." Root causes (all TDD red→green): (1) **Approvals invisible cross-device** — the Tasks page's own `restoreFromSnapshot` was ADD-ONLY on known rows (it only added brand-new tasks), so a kid's tap on another device never reached an open parent's "Needs approval" queue (Home's badge used the richer store merge and DID count it — pointing at a page that showed nothing); no approve → no earn tx → points never landed, and the stale page then pushed itself back over the shared `tasks-snapshot`, reverting the tap for other devices. Fix: the page now delegates tasks+weekData to the shared proof-gated `mergeTasksSnapshot` (ref-mirrored current state, no stale closure; rewards/penalties legs unchanged). (2) **Re-approval after an undo silently paid nothing** — `approvePendingCompletion` treated ANY historical earn as already-paid even after an undo reversed it (toast said "Approved! +5pts", zero points moved); now reversal-aware, mirroring the claim route's `reversed` check. (3) **Stale previous-week snapshot resurrected old points** — weekData merge adopted ANY different week; after a Monday rollover an unrefreshed snapshot could overwrite the fresh week with last week's points then get re-archived/wiped by the week-reset loop; now only a snapshot week ≥ the local week is adopted (older refused, newer still adopted so a device asleep for two weeks catches up). Tests +8: NEW `tasks-points-bugs` (5) + `tasks-pending-restore` (3 page-level, jsdom); the outdated "any different week is adopted" contract in `task-utils-snapshot-merge` rewritten to pin the direction rule. Suite 1373/1373, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files. **User action: none — points recover on the next 60s sync cycle after devices reload once; if an approval seemed to vanish earlier, the tapped row may need one more approve.**
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-08 | **Schedules/week-data/chat/rewards PB writes un-dead — legacy required fields healed + Calendar schedule sync made honest.** Comprehensive backend check of the Calendar/Schedule pages found the user's schedule never reached PocketBase: the live `schedules` collection carried a legacy REQUIRED `userId` field the app never sends → every save 400'd, swallowed by `saveSched()`'s old fire-and-forget `.catch(() => {})`. Full audit of all 45 collections found the same drift on 8 more write paths that had been silently dead for months (live PB had 0 rows: schedules, week_data, week_archive, emergency_contacts, chat_messages, rewards, family_goals, hall_of_fame): `schedules.userId`, `events.userId`, `emergency_contacts.userId`, `week_data.userId`, `week_archive.userId`, `rewards.points`+`userId` (app field is `cost`), `penalties.userId`+`points`, `chat_messages.message` (routes send `content`), `family_goals.name`+`target` (app writes `title`/`targetPoints`), `hall_of_fame.memberId`+`title`, `meal_plan_entries.time`. Fixes: (1) `pb-seed.ts` — every legacy field now declared `required: false` in the seed (so both fresh instances and self-heal produce the right shape) + the **required-drift heal extended to ALL field types** (was text-only via the options.max branch; now `s.required !== undefined && liveField.required !== drift` returns a drift entry for any type) — `npm run pb:seed` demoted every live field (verified: "ALL CLEAN" re-audit + live POST with the exact app payload returns 200); schedules also gained the missing `mealType` field (the form picker never persisted). (2) `src/lib/schedule-time.ts` (NEW, pure) — `scheduleTimeMinutes` (12h+24h), `formatScheduleTime12h` (replaces the `new Date("2000-01-01T"+time)` path that rendered **Invalid Date** for every 12-hour schedule on Home's Daily Schedule widget), `scheduleCoversWeekday` (the old `days.includes(weekday)` never matched "weekdays"/"weekends" — those routines silently vanished from Home), `scheduleDedupeKey`. (3) Calendar page (`src/app/calendar/page.tsx`) — schedule saves now `saveOrQueue` with honest toasts ("⚠️ saved on this device — will sync automatically"), PB `schedules` pulled on mount + on the 60s `consuela-data-refreshed` pulse with the same server-id-adoption merge contract as events (`dbScheduleToScheduleItem` mapper in `google-mapping.ts`); `ScheduleItem.id`/`editingSchedId` widened to `number | string`. (4) `pending-writes.ts` + `push-local-to-pb.ts` — schedules joined the offline queue (create/update/delete runner branch) and the Settings push dedupes by title+time+days. (5) Read paths (`pb-db.ts` + `db/index.ts`) fixed with the shared helpers. Tests +16 (schedule-time 12, seed-heal +5 incl. required-demote red-proofs, schedule-mapper 6, pending-writes-schedules 3); suite 1339/1339, tsc clean, eslint 0 new (1 pre-existing calendar set-state-in-effect), build clean. **User action: redeploy + your last-night schedule still lives only in the device you typed it on — tap Settings → Data & sync → "Push local data to cloud" once to migrate it.**

Geometry probe now reports **0 overlaps** (phone+desktop, dark+light, both tabs). Lesson: the old overlap probe compared card BODY rects only — touching cards (gap 0) don't intersect, and the absolute icon layers aren't card rects; icon-vs-previous-card intersection is now part of the check. Tests +1 (`tasks-polish-copy` rhythm assertion), suite 1183/1183, `verify-tasks-polish.mjs` 28/28, tsc clean.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-04 | **Consuela polish round 2 — multi-calendar Google sync + calendar avatar truth + PIN error honesty (fixA/fixB/fixC + tasks-pin lanes).** Google Calendar now syncs every selected calendar: new PB collection `consuela_google_calendar_sync` (per-calendar token/state/selection, first-run migration from the legacy single token), composite `(calendar_id, google_id)` unique index with normalize→dedupe→swap self-heal in `ensureGoogleCollections`, per-calendar sync loop (failure isolation, per-calendar 410→full-resync, one bulk fetch per calendar replacing 2 round trips/event, deselect-race guard re-checked before work and again before the token save), NEW `/api/google/calendars` GET (any session) / PUT (adult-gated via `authorizeAdminRequest`), Settings calendar multi-select with colorRgb dots + honest error/Try-again, events tinted with their own calendar's color (fallback cyan), tasks scope dropped from device-auth defaults (Device Flow rejects it). PIN honesty: `verifyPinRemote` returns `ok | wrongPin | unreachable` — KidHome (quest claim gained a catch — no silent offline no-op), RewardsShop and ALL SEVEN Tasks gates now distinguish "Couldn't reach Consuela" from "Wrong PIN", PIN cleared on both paths. Ops: the new collection, token migration, and events-index heal run automatically via `ensureGoogleCollections` (first Google API request or the 5-min cron) — `npm run pb:seed` does NOT cover them. Fix: keyframe is now opacity+scale only (globals.css). **Critical 2 (contrast):** the card background is the SKY stack but text ink stayed `skin.ink` (tuned for the OLD WeatherSkins skies) — night bottom `#c7b6e6` under white ink ≈1.9:1, storm `#5b5480` under dark ink ≈2.7:1. Fix (both directions): storm wash deepened `#6a6f96→#4d4770` (white ink passes AA at every stop), night wash LIGHTENED `#6f74a8→#e2dbf2` (slate-800 ink `#1E293B` passes everywhere), and card text ink now derives from `heroScene` (storm=white, else slate-800) in the `skin` memo — `INK` token map shipped in wx-tokens for reference; `GLASS_NIGHT` re-tuned from 12% to 40% white frost (the lightened night sky no longer needs a dark-tinted chip); the radial scrim now keys on `heroScene==="storm"` not `skin.night`; every `skin.night ?` text branch swapped to `heroScene === "storm"`. Tests assert night ink `rgb(30,41,59)` + storm ink `rgb(255,255,255)`. **Important:** (1) strip hours beyond the visible width were unreachable on touch (`touchAction: "pan-y"` disables browser h-panning while the slider consumes gestures) — DayStrip now auto-`scrollBy`s the selected cell into view on preview change (centered, clamped to range); (2) wear line was functionally dead (`outlook ?? wearLine` — outlook is always set) — restored as a SECOND line under the outlook; (3) touch-target regressions: Details 32→44px, Back-to-now 26→36px + `.hit-44`; (4) `Moon`'s `sky` prop was never passed (bite color matched no backdrop) — crescent is now a CSS `mask-image` radial bite (earthshine disc at 28% under a bright masked crescent; no background knowledge needed); (5) teach-demo's inner 1400ms revert could stomp a user's pin and leaked on unmount — now guards on `previewPinnedRef` (mirrored via a `setPinned` wrapper used by tap/release) and clears BOTH timers. **Minors:** lightning flash storm-only (was any `isWet` — drizzle no longer invents severity); storm `Condition` 1.1→1.0 height (83.6px icon no longer bleeds out of the 76px box); dead "Next hours" kicker ternary removed (stripEndIndex truncates at the day boundary, so it could never fire); modal `mBirds` gained the card's `!fetchError` gate (birds no longer fly over stale data; `fetchError` prop threaded into WeatherDetailsModal). **Tests:** +2 (mid-strip tap mapping with mocked getBoundingClientRect — restores the STRIP_PITCH-division contract the x=9999 change masked; night-ink assert), sky hex asserts updated to the new tokens, moon 2-div contract kept. Verified: weather 59/59, suite 1187/1187, tsc clean, eslint clean on touched files.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-03 | **Cross-device sync honesty sweep + photo-avatar propagation + PB `store` field — the "phone edits missing on the iPad" triage.** User reported: meals/recipes created on the phone (Tailscale) missing on the iPad, profile photos not propagating, and "Consuela suggests" showing "letterings not rendering correctly". Live investigation (PB admin dumps + signed-in gateway probes + 3 parallel subagent code traces) found the data WAS in PB (16 meals, 1 recipe, photo emojis on 3 members — the phone's push had worked) — the bugs were all in the read/report layers, plus 2 silent PB schema bugs: **(1) Fake push success** — `src/lib/push-local-to-pb.ts` counted `null`-returning writes (401-swallowed via safeGatewayRow) as `pushed++`, and `track()` incremented the PREVIOUS collection's error entry (TypeError on the first) — a fully-signed-out push showed "Pushed N items" while pushing nothing. Rewritten: result-checked counting, honest errors. **(2) Silent stale-cache fallback** — when a device's session 401'd, `useMeals`/`useRecipes` fell back to stale localStorage with no hint. New `db.gatewayReadStatus()` + `syncBlocked` state; PlanTab + RecipeBox show the Tasks-style sign-in hint when blocked+empty. Also 4 meal-creation paths (addRecipeToPlan, addRecipeToMealSlot, copyDayMeals, duplicateMeal) showed unconditional "Added" toasts — now honest via new pure `mealSaveToast()` helper. **(3) Photos frozen at mount** — every avatar surface except PlanTab captured the roster once at mount with no `consuela-members-updated` listener; the 60s CacheRefresher updated the cache but the UI never re-rendered. Fix: the `membersVersion` listener pattern (PlanTab's) added to Home family strip, Tasks, Chat speaker picker, KidHome, and Calendar member chips (the chips' dead `consuela-members` storage listener — a key nothing ever wrote — replaced by the live event via new `src/lib/calendar-member-snapshot.ts`). **(4) The "letterings"** — Tasks AI-suggestion card, DailyQuestCard, and MemberSheet rendered `assigneeEmoji` as RAW TEXT, and photo members now carry 100-226KB base64 data-URLs -> base64 dumped as text. All three now render `<Avatar variant="emoji">`. **(5) PB had no `grocery_list_items.store` field** — Shop tab store assignments were silently dropped (root of the wrong "3 items have no store assigned" suggestion); seed now carries `store` (+ `priority` option heal) + `grocery_store_optimization` added to the `proactive_suggestions.kind` select + the field-drift heal extended to select values (exact-match); live NAS healed ("patched +1 fields: store"). **(6) Gateway sanitizer** `/pin|secret|password|token/i` stripped the grocery `pinned` manual-override field from every write — replaced with a precise credential-key matcher (`pinned` survives). **(7) Engine undefined-guards** — pantry/penalty/calendar-conflict scanners could interpolate literal "undefined" + a double-space; fixed with name fallbacks ("Pantry item", "A family member", "Untitled event"). Suite 878/878 (+66), tsc clean, eslint clean on touched files (2 pre-existing baseline findings). Deployed as `sync-honesty-1` + live-verified: store/PIN round-trips, 16 meals + 1 recipe visible signed-in, guest reads honestly 401, Playwright — photo avatars render as <img> on Home (4)/Tasks (3)/Meals (3), zero raw base64 in any DOM, zero page errors, all phone-created meals visible on their correct days.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-02 | **Profile-photo bug sweep — kids' PINs, the crop editor's dead Apply button, and Tasks-page photo avatars.** Three bugs surfaced while testing the reposition editor, all root-caused with live repro (browser hit-testing + PB inspection) and fixed TDD. **(1) "Kids' PINs are wrong"** — the PINs were never wrong: the universal-task claim modal defaulted "Claim for" to the FIRST non-pet member (Rebecca), so a kid typing their own PIN verified against Mom → "Wrong code for selected member". Fix: new pure `pickDefaultClaimMember(members, signedInName)` in `src/lib/task-utils.ts` (first-name-insensitive match, pets excluded) wired into `openPinEntry` in `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` — the claim now defaults to the signed-in member. Permission audit confirmed correct: `/api/members/profile` + `/pin` are self-service (actor's own PIN, own record only); `/api/members/admin` writes stay adults-only (kid PATCH → 401). **(2) Photos never saved from the profile sheet** — the PhotoCropEditor rendered INSIDE the inline ProfileSheet/Settings Modal, which lives in PageShell's `relative z-10` <main> stacking context — the z-50 CapsuleNav painted over the bottom row and swallowed the Apply/Cancel taps (browser-verified: Apply's hit-test returned the nav's Calendar button on desktop AND phone). Fix: `src/components/profile/PhotoCropEditor.tsx` now renders via `createPortal(…, document.body)` (same escape RecipeModal uses), so the full-screen editor sits above the nav — Apply works, `POST /api/members/profile` 200s, the photo lands in PB. **(3) Tasks page showed 👤 instead of photos** — `memberEmojis` ran every member emoji through `safeDisplayEmoji` (a TEXT-label sanitizer that maps data URLs to 👤) before handing it to `<Avatar variant="emoji">`, which renders photos natively — so member tiles + leaderboard hid every photo avatar. Fix: `memberEmojis` carries the raw emoji (Avatar handles data URLs via SigmaImage); `safeDisplayEmoji` stays only in `memberOptionLabel` (select-option text). New tests: `claim-default-member` (4), `photo-crop-editor-portal` (1), `tasks-member-photo-avatars` (2). Verified: suite 825/825, typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files (the 1 `<img>` no-img warning in the editor is the documented data-URL pattern), live browser repro — Caspian login → claim modal defaults to Caspian → upload → Apply → save 200 → PB holds the photo (test data restored to 🧒 afterwards).
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-02 | **Ask Consuela chat-speed sweep — replies now stream token-by-token and the dead pre-flight round trip is gone.** A single message used to pay: a dead `/api/chat/process` pre-flight (phantom-collection reads that always returned nothing), a guaranteed 2s thinking floor, up to 4 serial buffered LLM rounds with no timeout, uncached Hermes config reads on every call, fetch-whole-collection tool scans, serial persist writes, and a full daily-thread re-fetch. Fixes (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-02-consuela-chat-speed-design.md`): (1) `src/components/chat/UnifiedInput.tsx` sends straight to `/api/hermes/chat` — the dead clarification stack is deleted (`/api/chat/process` route, `src/lib/consuela-ai-enhanced.ts`, `ClarificationModal`, orphaned `ConsuelaFAB`, unused `buildSystemPrompt`); (2) thinking floor 2000ms→400ms and streamed replies bypass it entirely; (3) Hermes config cached 10min + 60s `AbortSignal.timeout` per call + parallel persist; (4) opt-in SSE streaming end-to-end — the route streams content deltas + per-tool `status` events with parallel tool rounds and buffered auto-fallback if Hermes doesn't stream; shared client `src/lib/chat-stream.ts`; the chat page renders tokens live with a status line under the typing dots; Clem (grocery assistant) streams identically; sends gated on a stream-in-flight ref; (5) suggestion-engine scanners + briefing reads run concurrently; (6) hot tool handlers use PB-side filters + single-read batched grocery adds (with in-call dedupe); (7) incremental since-based thread reconcile (`/api/chat/messages?since=`); (8) Telegram mirror cron 30→5 min (`scripts/consuela/host-crontab.example` + registry text — applying it on the NAS host crontab is a user ops step). New probe `scripts/consuela/verify-chat-speed.mjs`. Verified: suite 815/815, typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files, verify-chat-speed.mjs ALL CHECKS PASSED (first paint 924ms).
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-02 | **Meals Consuela logs now land on the family's real days — the UTC/Eastern day-resolution bug is fixed end-to-end.** Symptom: the user told Consuela (Tuesday 9/1 evening) "yesterday was pizza dinner, today was leftover," but the meal planner showed it Wed/Thu. Root cause (systematic, live-PB verified): the dashboard container runs in UTC (no `TZ` set in `docker-compose.yml`) while the family is America/Detroit, so "today" flips to the next day at ~8 PM Eastern; worse, the Hermes agent had **zero date grounding** (the system prompt never stated the current date) and the read tools reported the UTC weekday/date (`src/lib/hermes-tools.ts` `todayISO()` = `new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]`, `get_dashboard_summary` weekday via UTC), so a Tuesday-evening conversation mapped to Wed/Thu. Fixes (TDD): (1) `src/lib/local-date.ts` gained TZ-aware helpers — `familyTimeZone()`, `localWeekdayShort()`, `localWeekStartISO()`, `localDateContext()` (+ tests); (2) new `src/lib/consuela-prompts.ts` extracts the Hermes system prompts and appends an explicit "Today is {weekday}, {date} ({tz}) / Yesterday was {weekday}, {date} / this week's Monday is…" block, so the model no longer guesses (route.ts thread-id `todayISO()` deliberately stays UTC per §3.5); (3) `src/lib/hermes-tools.ts` — `todayISO()` now returns the local date (which also fixes `add_event` defaults), `get_dashboard_summary` matches meals by local weekday and reports `today_weekday`+`family_timezone`, `get_weekly_meals` reports `today`+`current_week_monday`; (4) new `add_meal` tool — family-day meal logging (day = weekday short or YYYY-MM-DD; upserts day+mealType+week) so it goes through the controlled path; (5) `docker-compose.yml` sets `TZ=America/Detroit` (plus a NAS runbook note that the hermes-agent-2 container needs it too). Live data repaired: `Little Caesars Pizza` moved Wed→Tue 9/1, `Pizza Leftovers` moved Thu→Wed 9/2 (script `scripts/consuela/fix-meal-days-2026-09-02.mjs`, `from`-guard enforced; 3 sibling rows left untouched per user). Verified: typecheck clean, full suite 796/796, eslint clean on touched files.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-02 | **Profile photos can now be repositioned (Facebook-style drag + zoom) before saving.** After the centered-square crop landed (below), users wanted to choose *where* the crop view sits. New `src/components/profile/PhotoCropEditor.tsx` — a full-screen modal showing the photo in a square viewport: drag to pan, zoom via mouse wheel / pinch / −/+ buttons (1×–3×), pan clamped so the image always covers the frame, Esc/Cancel to abort. On Apply it maps the visible region back to the source rect (`getCropSourceRect`) and draws it to a 256×256 canvas → WebP. `src/components/profile/AvatarPicker.tsx` — file pick now resizes the raw photo to ≤1024px (aspect-preserving) as the editor source (`resizeImageToDataUrl`), opens the editor, and only calls `onChange(cropped256)` on Apply; emoji/"Use emoji" clears any pending crop. Public API (`value`/`onChange`/`fallbackEmoji`) unchanged, so both the Home profile sheet and Settings → Family Members work as-is. Pure, testable crop math exported: `coverScale`/`clampPan`/`getCropSourceRect` (+ new `tests/unit/avatar-crop.test.ts` 12 tests). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-02-avatar-reposition-editor-design.md`. Verified: typecheck clean; eslint clean on touched files (one intentional `<img>` no-img warning for the data-URL crop, same pattern as `SigmaImage`); avatar-crop 12/12; full suite 753/754 (1 pre-existing hermes-port failure).
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-01 | **Photo avatars are now centered-square cropped on upload — no more blurry or cut-off faces in the Home header/family strip.** Symptom: a photo added for Rebecca rendered blurry / with the face cut off in the small circular avatars. Root cause (systematic): `src/components/profile/AvatarPicker.tsx` `fileToDataUrl` resized to ≤256px **preserving the source aspect ratio** (e.g. a landscape selfie → 256×144), and `src/components/ui/SigmaImage.tsx` renders those in a small circle with `object-cover` (center crop) — so landscape faces got cropped top/bottom and wide-shot faces stayed tiny (blurry) inside the 28–40px circles. Fix: a centered-square crop — exported `centerCropSquare(srcW, srcH)` picks the min-dimension square, and `drawImage` maps it to a square canvas capped at 256 (native size below that, so low-res uploads aren't upscaled) before WebP export (subject fills the circle, sharp and correctly framed). Both the Home profile sheet and Settings → Family Members share `AvatarPicker`, so both surfaces benefit. New `tests/unit/avatar-crop.test.ts` (4: landscape/portrait/square/wide-landscape). Verified: avatar-crop 4/4, typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-02 | **Weather widget critique round 3 — all P1/P2s closed: tappable recovery, snow severity, severity-wins decor rule, type floor, tap-to-pin.** Follow-up to the 27/40 critique. (1) **Try-again regression fixed** — the failure-banner button computed `pointer-events: none` inside the content overlay (browser-verified: taps hit the decorative fog layer); banner + button now carry `pointer-events-auto`. (2) **Snow severity** — `WeatherSkins.severeFamily()` extends severity beyond storms: heavy-snow codes 73/75/85/86 get their own day treatment (sky pulled toward slate `#8B99B5/#7C8BA3`, ice-blue accent `#7FA8D9`) + new `snowAdvice()` pill "Big snow today — boots by the door" (kid: "Big snow! Boots and mittens today") with the same clearing-time logic (`severeEndISO` = first hour outside the current severe family). (3) **Severity wins the card** — `resolveAccent(skin, holidayAccent)` refuses the holiday party accent when severe, and SeasonHolidayArt + HolidayParticles are suppressed while `skin.severe` (Halloween no longer throws a party on a storm card). (4) **11px type floor** — the 8px "↔ drag" chip replaced by a grip-dots icon, holiday badge 9→11px, strip SVG labels 10→11px, preview temp badge 9.5→11px (pill enlarged), modal kickers/chip labels 10→11px. (5) **Tap-to-pin preview** — a tap (no 6px horizontal intent) now *pins* the previewed hour instead of doing nothing; pinned state shows a larger thumb + "↩ Back to now" chip in the kicker row; drag still glides home after 650ms; Escape/Home release; blur keeps a pinned answer on the card. (6) **Fusion promoted to the pill grammar** — "Rain around Soccer Practice" now renders as a tinted rounded card (headline + detail) instead of a third flat text line, and the wear line cedes while fusion shows (one stronger answer, not both). (7) **Teach-by-demonstration** — once per mount, after first data lands (skipped under reduced-motion), the scene auto-previews ~hour 6 for 1.4s then glides home, introducing the signature gesture. Fusion now testable: `db.selectTodaysEvents` mocked via `vi.hoisted` seam in the test file. Verified: typecheck clean (pre-existing hermes-tools-dates errors are another workstream's untracked files), suite 777/777 (weather 51/51, +7), redesign probe 30/30 + features 16/16 live, detector: only the 2 documented `--ease-spring` warnings.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-02 | **Weather widget hardened + made Consuela's own — a11y, honest failure, storm severity, calendar fusion.** Full critique-driven pass (25/40 → targeted all 5 P1/P2s + minors). **Trust/a11y:** the card's `role="img"` (which flattened its own unit toggle / slider / Details button for screen readers — ARIA makes descendants of role=img presentational) is now `role="group"` + aria-label so the controls speak; a single sr-only `role="status" aria-live="polite"` region announces hero state on data refreshes only (the old hero-wide live region re-announced the whole block on every scrub step); the Details modal has a real focus trap (Tab cycles inside, focus lands on Close, focus returns to the Details trigger on close) and its Close button is 44px; the dead chevron rotation is gone; the Details + unit-toggle hit areas are ≥44px (toggle now shows the TARGET unit "°C" as an action, not status); precip % chips are conditionally rendered instead of `opacity:0` (invisible-but-announced); holiday particles never stop under reduced-motion — `weatherConfettiFall`/`weatherShamrockFloat`/`weatherHarvestFloat`/`weatherSnowAccumulate` joined the globals.css kill block. **Honest under failure/degradation:** every fabricated fallback is gone — missing temp/feels/humidity/wind/cloud render "—", hidden rows, or dropped curve points (no more fake 60°/50% that a parent dresses kids by); the Open-Meteo fetch has a 12s AbortController timeout (a hung request can no longer pin `inFlightRef` and silently kill every 15-min refresh) and treats aborts as background failures; hourly/sunrise/sunset naive ISO strings are parsed in the LOCATION's `utc_offset_seconds` and normalized to true UTC (a parent's phone in another timezone no longer shifts the hourly axis); the failure banner gained a real tappable "Try again" wired to `loadWeather`; the empty 5-day forecast renders an honest message instead of a bare box. **Severity at a glance:** `getWeatherSkin` now applies a day-side storm modifier (code ≥95) — pastel sky desaturated toward storm gray, storm-amber accent, gray clouds — so a 2 PM thunderstorm no longer renders as a cheerful lemon card; `deriveOutlookInfo` escalates to "Thunderstorms expected around 4 PM", detects the first non-storm hour after the current storm ("stormEndISO"), and a reassurance pill shows "⛈️ Thunderstorms — inside is best right now · Clearing by around 6 PM" (kid copy: "Storms right now — stay inside"); the night moon now rides the real arc opposite the sun (was pinned at 76%/16%). **Consuela's night:** the (Not Boring) near-black `#060609→#141420` night card (a hole in the warm-glass bento, invisible in a dark kitchen) is re-tinted to lamplit indigo `#1B1E33→#2A2440` with softer cloud/border tones — white numerals unchanged; night sky layers now render from `skin.skyGradient()` so WeatherScene no longer duplicates the gradients (single source in WeatherSkins.ts). **Family language:** new `src/lib/weather-insights.ts` (pure, unit-tested) — `wearAdvice` ("Coats this morning"/"Grab a coat"/"Raincoats ready" under the H/L line, kid-aware via role=child), `fusionOutlook` ("Rain around Soccer Practice — should pass before Movie Night at 7:00 PM": the next ≥50% precip hour matched against today's family events from `db.selectTodaysEvents()` ±75min, parsing both "16:00" and "4:00 PM" times, shown only when they line up), `stormAdvice`; day-strip labels 8.5px→10px, scrubber 9px→10px, "Rest of today" kicker 9px→11px. **Cleanup:** dead duplicate `src/components/weather/` tree deleted (particles/icons/StatPill/helpers/Backdrops/Overlays — zero importers, superseded by the ui/ WeatherScene family). Probes updated (`[role="img"]`→`[role="group"]` selectors, night rgb(27,30,51), hero `data-testid="wx-hero-temp"`); detector now clean except the 2 documented project-standard `--ease-spring` bounce-easing warnings; the page-wide "hero 75% occluded by svg" overlay finding was investigated: geometric overlap is real but the SVGs are decorative scene layers — `elementsFromPoint` hit-testing confirms pointer/visual reads fine, no z-order defect. Tests: +8 (storm sky/copy, clearing announcement, retry, honest-null hero, abort-timeout, a11y group/live-region) +10 insights/skins unit tests, suite 741/742 (1 pre-existing hermes-port failure), typecheck clean, eslint clean, redesign probe 30/30 + features probe 16/16 in a real browser.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-31 | **Guest "vanishing tasks" made honest — the Tasks page now says "sign in" instead of silently showing an empty list.** Symptom: tasks created in the afternoon appeared gone in incognito/fresh browsers. Root cause was never data loss — all 7 rows lived safely in PB (`tasks` + `consuela_data_snapshots`); the session-auth migration gates every `/api/db/*` and `/api/tasks/sync` read behind the `consuela_session` cookie, so signed-out browsers get 401s and the Tasks page silently fell back to empty local cache with the "🎉 All caught up" empty state (a compounding window earlier in the day also had the Secure-cookie-over-HTTP regression, fixed separately). Fix (TDD): `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` — the mount restore now records `guestSyncBlocked` when `/api/tasks/sync` returns 401, and the Pending empty state renders an honest 🔐 "Tasks are synced to the family account — Sign in with your PIN to see everyone's tasks" EmptyState when `!isLoggedIn && guestSyncBlocked && tasks.length === 0`; signed-in genuinely-empty lists and guests with local tasks keep the old copy. New `tests/unit/tasks-guest-sync-state.test.tsx` (3: guest-401 shows hint, signed-in-empty keeps "All caught up", local tasks show without the hint). Verified: suite 693/693, typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-01 | **Composio integration fixed + honest connection dots.** The Composio key Jeffery saved 2026-08-29 was encrypted with a `CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY` matching neither local dev nor the NAS container (split-brain after container recreate) → `decryptSecret` failed silently → "Instacart integration is not enabled." Three additional compounding bugs found and fixed: (1) `createShoppingListViaComposio` parsed the wrong v3.1 response shape (`data.result.output.shopping_list_url` vs the real `data.url`); (2) the Composio Test button probed a dead v1 endpoint (410 → showed green "reachable" for any key including invalid ones); (3) no distinction between "no key stored" and "stored but unreadable" in the status dots. Fixes: honest v3.1 test (`v3.1/tools?limit=1` with 401/403/410 = red), response parsing updated to handle `data.url` + legacy shapes, `unreadable` flag on `ServiceFieldStatus` for corrupt ciphertext, auto-test on Settings card mount + "Test all" button, "Saved — unreadable (re-enter)" dot label for broken secrets. Local `.env.local` encryption key aligned with NAS to prevent future split-brain. Composio key re-stored via live dashboard API. Verified live: Instacart POST returns URL, composio test `ok=true`, dots honest. +10 new tests.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-01 | **Calendar dummy data removed + events made persistent.** The Calendar page still shipped the original 10 hardcoded demo events (Soccer Practice, Piano Lesson, Dentist, Movie Night…) as its localStorage fallback + pre-mount render, so every fresh/cleared browser re-seeded them and they persisted in `consuela-events` forever. Fixes: (1) `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — the demo array is gone (`const events: CalEvent[] = []`); (2) **manual events now persist cross-device** — the page loads PB `events` via `gatewayList("events")` on mount (merging + adopting server ids for local rows), and add/edit/delete go through `saveOrQueue` with honest toasts (was fire-and-forget `.catch(() => {})` with a number-only update gate that never matched PB string ids); (3) **pending-writes queue extended to events** (`src/lib/pending-writes.ts` collection union + runner branch, +3 tests); (4) Google sync now replaces the Google rows even when the server returns 0 events (stale cached events can't linger); (5) data epoch bumped to 2 in `SyncInit.tsx` — wipes the dummy-poisoned `consuela-events` cache on every device (Google events re-sync from the server, family events load from PB); (6) deleted the dead original seeder `scripts/seed-pb.ts` (the source of the demo events; not wired to any npm script). New `dbEventToCalEvent` mapper in `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts` (+3 tests). Verified live: PB `events` 0 rows, `consuela_google_calendar_events` 67 rows (Google data is server-persistent, cron-synced every 5 min). Suite 728/729 (1 pre-existing hermes-port failure), typecheck clean, eslint: only the pre-existing calendar set-state-in-effect.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-09-01 | **Cross-origin/cross-browser meals+recipes write reliability — no more silent data loss.** Root cause: all meal/recipe writes silently swallowed 401 (no session cookie on a different origin) and showed fake "✅ added!" toasts. Fixes (TDD): (1) **Pending-writes queue** (`src/lib/pending-writes.ts`, 8/8 tests) — localStorage-backed `PendingWrite` queue (`meal:create/meal:update/meal:delete/recipe:create/recipe:update/recipe:delete` keys) exposed via `saveOrQueue(op, fallbackFn)` which tries the real DB call and queues on failure; `flushPendingWrites` retries queued writes until one succeeds. (2) **Honest toasts** in useMeals/saveMeal/deleteMeal/generateWeeklyPlan/archiveCurrentWeek and useRecipes/saveCatalogRecipe/deleteCatalogRecipe — on failure shows "⚠️ saved on this device — will sync automatically" instead of fake success. (3) **Event-driven re-merge** — `db.refreshCaches()` now dispatches a `consuela-data-refreshed` CustomEvent; useMeals and useRecipes listen for it and re-merge PB data into local state without a page reload. (4) **All un-persisted paths wired** — meals/page.tsx `addRecipeToPlan`, `addRecipeToMealSlot`, `copyDayMeals`, `duplicateMeal`, `saveRecipe` (both add and edit branches) all now `await saveOrQueue(...)` with honest toasts; `action-runner.ts` Consuela chat meal-creation tool also uses `saveOrQueue`. (5) **Archive restore** (`meals/archive/page.tsx`) wrapped with `saveOrQueue`. (6) **Flush on sign-in** — `useAuth.login` now calls `flushPendingWrites().then(() => db.refreshCaches())` so queued writes land immediately after session establishment. (7) **CacheRefresher** flushes pending writes before every cache refresh. (8) **Meals push dedupe** — `push-local-to-pb.ts` now skips meals whose name+weekOf already exist server-side (prevents duplicate rows when re-running Phase 1 recovery). (9) **Dead code removed** — `src/lib/sync-service.ts` and `src/lib/sync-data.ts` (both pointed to unreachable `100.120.64.66:6789`, zero active importers besides SyncInit) deleted; `SyncInit.tsx` cleaned up (keeps epoch reset, drops dead merge/push calls). New tests: `tests/unit/pending-writes.test.ts` (8), `tests/unit/meal-recipe-autosave.test.tsx` (7), `tests/unit/push-local-dedupe.test.ts` (2). Verified: suite 722/722 (1 pre-existing hermes port failure), typecheck clean, eslint clean on all touched files.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-31 | **Profile photo upload fixed — PocketBase `members.emoji` was silently capped at 5000 chars.** Root cause (systematic debugging + live repro): this PocketBase build treats a text field's `max: 0` as the built-in 5000-char default (the live field's schema even reported `max: 0`), so every real photo avatar save — a 256px webp data URL, ~8–60KB of base64 — was rejected by PB with `validation_max_text_constraint` → the `/api/members/profile` route 500'd `Failed to update profile`. Emoji saves worked because short strings fit; the UI symptom was "can't save the profile with a photo, but emoji saves fine" (both the Home profile sheet and Settings → Family Members photo edits). Fix: `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` — `members.emoji` now carries an explicit `options: { max: 400000 }` (matching the profile route's `MAX_AVATAR_CHARS`, now exported), and `seedCollections()` gained a **field-option heal** that patches existing text fields whose `max` drifted from the seed on the next `npm run pb:seed`; the live NAS PB was healed the same run (`members (patched +1 field options (emoji))`). New migration `pb_migrations/1788223651_updated_members.js` for fresh instances. +4 tests (`tests/unit/pb-seed-field-heal.test.ts`). Verified: suite 690/690, typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files; live end-to-end (Playwright, signed in as Rebecca) — a noisy photo producing a **50,751-char** data URL now saves (`Saved ✓`, PB holds the data URL), where 5,000+ chars previously 500'd; route-level 250,000-char payloads return 200.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-31 | **Backend wiring sweep repaired — Clem persona on Hermes, meal-suggest parsing, Instacart buy, guest AI exemptions, ✨ Generate, hygiene, functional Settings emergency Test.** (1) **Clem = scoped Consuela persona** on the grocery page: `src/app/api/hermes/chat/route.ts` accepts `{system, agent}`; `agent:"clem"` uses a 7-tool grocery allowlist + `CLEM_SYSTEM_PROMPT`, skips chat persistence, and the fallback URL was fixed `:8643`→`:8642` (no new Hermes profile); `src/components/meals/ClemAssistant.tsx` now sends `agent:"clem"` + last 6 messages. (2) **Meal suggest** parsed `data.actions` but the chat route never returned it — new `src/lib/ai-response.ts:extractActions()` parses `data.content` (actions / meal_plan / prose shapes) and every caller (useMeals / useRecipes / tasks page ×2) was fixed; `useMeals.ts` also gained `generateWeeklyPlan(weekOf, overwrite)` wired to the new **✨ Generate** button in `src/components/meals/PlanTab.tsx`. (3) **Guest AI exemptions:** `src/middleware.ts` `API_EXEMPT` 6→9 (added `/api/hermes/`, `/api/consuela/suggestions`, `/api/consuela/briefing`) — guests get child-role (no house tools) Clem + suggestions; emergency-contacts stays gated. (4) **Instacart buy** now returns honest JSON 503 (config reads moved inside try/catch), per-store failures no longer abort the whole request, `COMPOSIO_API_KEY` plumbed through both compose files + `.env.example`, orphaned `InstacartConnectCard.tsx` deleted. - **Last Updated:** 2026-08-31 | **NAS deploy un-broken — Clem chat + TheMealDB recipe search/save fixed (3 root causes, mostly ops).** Symptoms were: "Instacart AI chat (Clem) not connecting", "TheMealDB service not connecting", "can't save recipes at all". Systematic debugging found three compounding root causes: **(1) Wrong Docker network** — the redeployed `consuela-dashboard` container landed on the default `bridge` network instead of `familydashboard_consuela-net`, so `hermes-agent-2` didn't resolve (Clem chat dead) and `192.168.0.28:8090` was unreachable (container logs: `ConnectTimeoutError … attempted address: 192.168.0.28:8090` → recipe search 502'd in ~10.5s and gateway writes 502'd). Fixed by recreating the container on `familydashboard_consuela-net` with `NEXT_PUBLIC_PB_URL=http://pocketbase:8090` (server-side only — the browser never uses this var) and `HERMES_API_URL=http://hermes-agent-2:8642` (current Hermes `API_SERVER_PORT=8642`; the old container's `:8643` is stale). **(2) Secure session cookie on plain HTTP** — `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts` set `secure: NODE_ENV === "production"`, so on `http://192.168.0.28:3000` browsers never SENT the cookie back: login returned 200 but every session-gated call 401'd (recipe saves, chat tools, gateway writes). Fix: new exported `sessionCookieSecure()` honoring `SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=false` (passed on the NAS container; default stays secure in production), +3 unit tests. **(3)** Confirmed non-bugs: recipe arrays are JSON-stringified by `db.upsertRecipe` before the gateway (direct curl without stringify looks like data loss but isn't the UI path), and guest 401s on gated endpoints are expected (UI falls back to local data). Verified live end-to-end (Playwright, signed in as Rebecca): TheMealDB search renders 25 chicken results, detail shows ingredients, "Save to catalog" persists the recipe to PB with full ingredients/tags, Clem FAB opens and Hermes replies. `DEPLOY_NAS_LOCAL.md` gotchas #11–13 added; `docker-compose.yml` gained `SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE`. Commit `0a8a75c`.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-31 | **Weather card stays live on its own — 15-minute auto-refresh + tab-wake refresh.** The widget no longer freezes at whatever the page loaded with: the Open-Meteo fetch is now a stable `loadWeather` callback that runs on mount, every 15 minutes, and immediately when the browser tab becomes visible again with data older than 10 minutes. Refreshes are silent — the card keeps showing current data while the new forecast loads (no skeleton flash; the temperature just counts to the new value when it lands), and a background failure keeps the stale data with no error banner (the "Weather unavailable" banner now only appears when there is no data at all, i.e., the initial fetch failed). An in-flight guard skips overlapping refreshes, and `dataLoadedRef`/`updatedAtRef` reset whenever the weather location changes so a failed first fetch for the new location correctly shows the banner instead of silently keeping the old city's data. `updatedAt` still only advances on success, so "Updated Xm ago" stays honest. The details modal stays live automatically (same `weatherData` state). Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-31-weather-live-refresh-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-31-weather-live-refresh.md`. Verified: typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files, suite 661/661 (+7 tests: 15-min poll, stale tab-wake refetch, fresh tab-wake no-op, silent in-flight refresh, background-failure keeps data, location-change banner reset, overlap guard).

- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-27 | **Birds fly around the sun on clear days — passing behind it as they go.** A small (Not Boring)-style detail: three tiny seagull silhouettes (two-arc SVG glyphs, 8–10px wide, `skin.ink` at 55% opacity) circle the sun on a flattened elliptical flight path — crossing left→right in front of the sun, then swinging around behind it where they fade out (opacity 0 on the far half of `wxBirdOrbit`, scaled down to 0.8 for distance) before re-emerging at the left side. Each bird flaps like a distant seagull: an SMIL `<animate attributeName="d">` morphs the wing path between the wings-up and wings-down shapes (0.9–1.1s per flap, spline-eased — SMIL instead of CSS `d` so it works in every browser). Flight periods 17/23/20s with negative delays staggering the flock. The layer lives inside the sun element, so the flock automatically tracks the sun arc and timeline scrubbing. Data-true gating: birds appear only when the sky is genuinely clear — day + `cloud_cover < 30` + no rain/snow/storm codes + no fog — and fade in/out over 1.2s as conditions change. Reduced motion renders static silhouettes at fixed positions with no flap (the animate element is not rendered). Test hook: `data-testid="wx-birds"`. - **Last Updated:** 2026-08-27 | **Weather scene completes the Not Boring trifecta — realtime visibility fog, procedural clouds, true moon phases.** The three (Not Boring) Weather signature features the scene was missing are now in. (1) **Realtime simulation completed:** the scene now fetches and renders real `visibility` (Open-Meteo current + hourly, free field) — fog banks derive from actual visibility meters (<8km scales fog opacity up to 0.55; <1km = dense), replacing the humidity≥82% proxy (kept only as a weak fallback when visibility data is absent; WMO fog codes 45/48 still trigger). (2) **Procedural clouds:** the 4 fixed SVG cloud shapes are gone — each cloud is now procedurally generated as a cluster of 4–9 overlapping ellipses from a seeded PRNG (mulberry32) keyed to the hour timestamp, so every hour's clouds are unique and stable within the hour; blob count/size grow with `cloud_cover`; scrubbing the timeline regenerates the sky's clouds for that hour with a 1.1s crossfade (`wxFadeIn`, previously unused). And yes — **tap the clouds to build your own**: tapping a visible cloud puffs a new blob into it at the tap point (`wxPuff` 0.9s grow-and-fade, max 3 concurrent puffs, reduced-motion disables the toy). Clouds are tappable because the card's content overlay is now `pointer-events-none` with `pointer-events-auto` only on the unit toggle, day strip, and Details button (modal hero overlay likewise). (3) **True moon phases:** the fixed crescent is replaced by a real synodic model (29.53058867-day month from the Jan 6 2000 18:14 UTC new-moon epoch) — the night moon renders tonight's actual illuminated fraction via a terminator path (waxing lit right, waning lit left, earthshine dim disc beneath), glow scales with illumination, and the modal's Daylight card gained a dotted-leader **Moon row** ("Full Moon · 100%"). New exports from `WeatherScene.tsx` for testing: `moonPhase`, `moonPhaseName`, `moonPathD`, `mulberry32`, `makeCloudSpec`. New Open-Meteo field: `visibility` (current + hourly). Verified: typecheck clean, eslint clean, suite 631/631 (+11 tests: moon model ×4, procedural clouds ×4, visibility fog ×2, night-scene moon path ×1), redesign probe ALL CHECKS PASSED, new deterministic feature probe `scripts/consuela/verify-weather-features.mjs` 11/11 (mocked Open-Meteo: visibility in fetch URL, 4 unique multi-blob clouds at 90% cover, tap-to-puff adds an animated blob, fog at 800m / none at 16km, moon path at forced night, modal Moon row, 0 page errors).

- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **/meals member-strip hydration mismatch fixed + signed-in custom avatars now land.** Root cause: the module-load hydrate in `db/index.ts` warms `membersCache` from PocketBase **on the server** (admin PB access), so SSR rendered the real PB members (Jeffery's customized 😎) while the client's first render still had an empty cache (the browser roster fetch via `/api/members/admin` is async and 401s for guests) and rendered the static fallback (👨) — PlanTab's pre-mount branch read `db.selectMembers()`, which stopped being deterministic once that PB warm-up landed. Fix: new `db.selectMembersFallback()` (never reflects the warmed cache) now backs PlanTab's pre-mount render, so server HTML and client hydration are identical; PlanTab also listens for `consuela-members-updated` — now dispatched by the client hydrate on a **successful** roster fetch only (a fallback-only cache must not fire it: useAuth treats a missing member as deleted and signs out) — so the strip swaps from fallbacks to the real family when the roster lands (before this the `[mounted]` memo froze at whatever the cache held at mount, so signed-in users never saw custom avatars in the strip). Guests keep the honest fallback family. Verified: typecheck clean, suite 592/592 (+3 tests: node-env SSR determinism + no-PB-leak `renderToString`, jsdom roster swap), eslint 0 on touched files; Playwright guest + signed-in at 390px — 0 hydration errors on `/meals` + all three legacy deep links, signed-in strip shows Jeffery's real 😎 after mount.

"Apply to all" sets the cheapest store as default for every item. Walmart items get a separate local list + walmart.com search link since Walmart is NOT on Instacart in Holland. Composio is the primary shopping list backend (4 tools: `create_shopping_list`, `add_items_to_shopping_list`, `get_shopping_list_details`, `remove_items_from_shopping_list`) with a direct API fallback. Consuela can now suggest `grocery_store_optimization` (category isle clustering across pinned stores, pantry-low + buy-again detection). - **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **Grocery→Pantry handoff fixed for guests + expired sessions (local-first kitchen writes).** Root cause: the session-auth migration gated all `/api/db/*` writes behind the `consuela_session` cookie, but the kitchen hooks still gated UI state on PB write success — guests got 401 on every write, so "Send N to pantry" failed all items and checked rows stayed stuck on the list; compounding, live PB `grocery_list_items.userId` was required while most create paths never sent it (grocery creates 400'd even signed-in). Fix: `usePantry` (addPantryItem now local-first — PB write best-effort, the item always lands in pantry state + localStorage; update/remove state-first; normalizeName guarded against missing names; nameless PB rows filtered on load), `useGrocery` (add/toggle/update/delete/override all optimistic-local + best-effort PB; deleteGroceryItem falls back to delete-by-name when the local numeric id doesn't exist in PB, so fallback items materialized by a toggle no longer resurrect on reload), `db/index.ts` + `pb-db.ts` default `userId: "demo"` on grocery create, hermes-tools chat grocery create sends userId, `pb-seed.ts` declares userId optional. Live PB patched: `grocery_list_items.userId` no longer required. No visual change — the kitchen tabs stay calm input surfaces. Verified: typecheck clean, suite 569/569, 0 new lint; Playwright guest + signed-in — "🥫 Sent 5 items to pantry", checked rows clear, items appear in pantry, PB grocery ends 0 rows / pantry 5.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **Calendar page restyled to match the Home widget glass.** Every major Calendar surface (hero greeting, month grid, Today panel, Upcoming panel, Family Routines panel, Add/Edit form cards, and the upcoming day cards) now uses the shared `WidgetCard` pastel-bento shell — the exact same material as the Home widgets — with per-card tones from the Home palette (month grid cool blue `#3b82f6`, Today + Routines ice blue `#22d3ee`, Upcoming mint `#10b981`, forms violet `#8b5cf6`, hero follows the chosen accent). Event rows frost with their own event color, routine cards with their routine color, and active member filter chips now glow in that member's own color instead of the global accent. The hardcoded-cyan Sync button now follows the accent. Month-grid day cells are now visible unselected glass tiles (tone-tinted fill + hairline tone border + inset top highlight in dark and light) instead of near-invisible 3% white, with a crisper accent border on Today and a white edge on the selected day. The member filter chips' emojis now sit on the same `Avatar` pill as the Home family strip (28px member-color circle + tone ring) instead of overflowing a bare 21.6px dot. The month grid now borrows Apple Calendar's signatures: a larger lighter month title with ghost chevrons flanking a rounded "Today" pill (‹ Today ›), today's date number inside a filled accent circle with a matching glow (tile tint softened to support it), and slightly bigger event dots (4.2px). When today is the selected day both signals stay visible: the selected tile becomes a clear translucent accent box (20–22% fill + white inner ring, no dense gradient) and the today circle keeps its solid accent fill with a white ring — day cells now carry `is-selected` and `is-today` together instead of either/or. The Calendar ⇄ Schedule tab pills now use a solid accent-gradient fill with white text + accent glow for the active tab (was a 24% accent wash that blended into the glass track), so the highlighted tab is unmistakable in dark and light. Google-synced events are now month-accurate: they carry month/year (new `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts`), the sync no longer drops events outside the current month, and the grid/Today/Upcoming panels only show each event in its own month — no more ghost copies when navigating months. Console hygiene fixed: the anti-FOUC theme script moved from a raw `<script dangerouslySetInnerHTML>` to `next/script strategy="beforeInteractive"` (React 19 no longer warns; still injected into the server HTML head and runs pre-hydration — verified data-theme set at domcontentloaded), and the Calendar page's localStorage-seeded events/schedules no longer cause a hydration mismatch (render uses the deterministic fallbacks until mount via a `useSyncExternalStore` mounted flag, then swaps to stored data). Verified: Home `.widget-card` and Calendar grid card are byte-identical in computed style in both dark and light; typecheck clean, 0 new lint, suite 560/560, no page errors or horizontal overflow at 390/1440, no hydration/script-tag console errors even with stored events differing from fallbacks.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **Backend audit follow-up — regression tests + dead demo cleanup.** Added `tests/unit/input-route-guards.test.ts` (5 tests) covering the formData content-type guards on `/api/ocr/extract`, `/api/voice/process`, `/api/photo/process`, plus `/api/email/forward` field validation and admin-client `members` lookup with missing `consuela_saved_locations` tolerated; red-green verified by temporarily reverting the route guards (4 failures) and restoring them (5 passes). Deleted confirmed-dead demo modules that referenced phantom collections: `src/lib/family-ai.ts`, `src/lib/hermes-tools-enhanced.ts`, `src/lib/morning-briefing.ts`, old `src/hooks/useMorningBriefing.ts`, `src/components/morning-briefing/`, and `src/components/family-ai/` (real Home briefing uses `src/components/briefing/hooks/useMorningBriefing.ts`; chat uses `/api/hermes/chat` + `hermes-tools.ts`). Fixed `src/db/features/seed.ts` to query `members` instead of phantom `users`. - **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **Comprehensive backend audit — gateway unblocked, unauthenticated-PB reads fixed, dead/dangerous routes removed.** Follow-up sweep across all 78 API routes found and fixed a second class of breakage beyond the Integrations repair. **Root cause #1 (biggest):** NO PB collection had the standard `created`/`updated` autodate fields, but the sessioned gateway `/api/db/[collection]` sorts by `-created` by default — so EVERY browser-side gateway read 400'd at PB → bare empty-body 500 → the dual-mode client silently fell back to stale local cache (cross-device sync dead for all gateway collections). Fix: `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` now injects `created`/`updated` autodate fields into every collection (new `withAutodate()` + shared `buildField()` used by both create and patch paths); re-ran `npm run pb:seed` → all 37 collections patched. Gateway routes also hardened to return parseable JSON errors (`db_error` 502 / `invalid_json` 400) instead of empty 500s. **Root cause #2:** several server libs still used the unauthenticated `getPB()` client, which 403s on every locked collection. New `getAuthedPB()` helper in `src/lib/pb-auth.ts` (same cached admin client/token refresh as `withAdmin`); switched `skill-tree.ts` (was 500 "Failed to load skill tree data"), `schedule-analytics.ts` (was 500 "Only superusers…"), `recurring-patterns.ts`, and `family-memory.ts` to it — all now return real data. `family-memory` also needed its collection seeded (new `consuela_family_memories`, 37th collection). **Root cause #3:** formData routes (`ocr/extract`, `voice/process`, `photo/process`) threw Next's raw 500 on non-multipart requests and queried a phantom `users` collection via unauthenticated client — now content-type-guarded (clean 400), `users`→`members`, `consuela_saved_locations` read wrapped in fallback, all on `withAdmin`. `email/forward` got the same `users`→`members` + `withAdmin` fix (now 200 end-to-end). **Removed:** legacy `/api/family` route + `family.json` (zero callers; leaked plaintext PINs and allowed unauthenticated arbitrary file writes) and the dead `/api/consuela/chat` stub + its test (unauthenticated PB, phantom `conversations`/`conversation_messages`, hardcoded TODO reply — real chat uses `/api/hermes/chat`). **chat/process hardened:** `/api/chat/process` (enhanced-AI clarification layer, called by `UnifiedInput` before `/api/hermes/chat`) 500'd on phantom `consuela_family_members`/`consuela_saved_locations`; now those reads fall back to `[]` and the ambiguity check is skipped when no family context loads, so it passes through cleanly (200, no clarification spam) and chat still flows to Hermes. **Known limitations (left as graceful degradation, not crashes):** money-mountain + time-capsule collections were never seeded and their schemas use `relation` fields to a nonexistent `users` collection — both degrade to empty 200s (data doesn't persist); the enhanced-AI family (`family-ai`/`consuela-ai-enhanced` context enrichment) is a parallel demo built on phantom collections and only its crash paths were fixed. Verified live: all GET routes 200 (or deliberate 404 on removed routes), gateway reads 200, skill-tree/schedule-analytics/recurring-patterns/family-memory return real data, family-memory create/read/delete round-trips. Suite 555/555, `tsc` clean, eslint clean on all touched files.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **Settings → Integrations backend repaired (live PB reseed + seed-script fix + graceful degradation).** The live PocketBase (192.168.0.28:8090, recreated 2026-08-21) was missing 14 collections the Integrations section depends on — `consuela_service_config`, `ha_notify_config`, `ha_notify_prefs`, `ha_mirror_state`, `ha_entities/areas/devices/automations`, skill-tree ×5, `recurring_patterns` — so every `/api/services/*` route 500'd (🔑 Services & Keys card dead, all Test buttons failed, `/api/services/runtime` 500 → weather fell back to hardcoded coords) and the 🔔 Notifications card showed "config store unreachable". Fix: (1) `scripts/pb-seed.mjs` rewritten as the thin tsx wrapper around the canonical `seedCollections()` in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` (36 collections, LOCKED_RULES admin-only enforcement, schema/index self-heal; auto-loads `.env.local` via `process.loadEnvFile` when PB vars aren't exported — the old standalone script only knew the original 10 collections and never applied the lock); (2) `npm run pb:seed` run against the live instance — 14 collections created, `events` patched +3 importance fields, `recipes` +sourceUrl, drifted rules re-locked on members/meals/events/tasks/contacts/schedules/rewards/penalties; (3) graceful degradation: `GET /api/services/config` now returns 503 `{services:[], error:"config_store_unreachable"}` instead of a bare empty 500 (🔑 card shows an honest one-line error when the store is down), `GET /api/services/runtime` returns 200 `{runtime:{}, error:...}` so widgets keep their defaults, `GET /api/ha/health` returns 200 `{ok:false, configured:false, reason:"not_configured"}` instead of throwing when HA_HOST/HA_TOKEN are absent. Verified live: config GET/PUT/DELETE round-trip, per-service Test buttons report honest results (themealdb ok; hermes/telegram/gmail/HA not_configured or unreachable), notify-targets warning gone, notify-test returns honest 400 `telegram_not_configured`, Google device-grant still returns real codes. Suite 557/557 (+3 tests: config 503 path, runtime empty path, ha health not_configured path), tsc clean, 0 new lint. **Note:** `.env.local` still has no HA_HOST/HA_TOKEN, GMAIL_*, TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID, LAT/LON — enter them in the 🔑 card when ready (encrypted in PB).
