(4) **desktop** (landscape OR width ≥ 1280px) = an auto-fit tiling grid — `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(360px, 1fr))` fits as many uniform columns as the viewport holds, all widgets visible at once, vertical scroll; `WIDGET_WIDTHS` deleted; `widgetSpanClass("desktop")` returns `""`; `homeFooterSpanClass("desktop")` = `col-span-full`; `tabletSpan(index, count)` stretches the lone last widget of an odd tablet count to `col-span-2`; every Home card now fills its grid row (`h-full`) so short cards (Ask Consuela) match their row partner's height | **CapsuleNav:** the persistent bottom tab bar is now a floating bottom-center glass capsule where the active item expands into a neon-lime pill (`CapsuleNav.tsx` replaces `BottomNav.tsx` — same 6 routes/items, `--capsule-accent: #78f05a`, CSS-only grid-column expansion, auto-scale on narrow viewports, reduced-motion safe). | **Three-mode Home layouts (phone / tablet / desktop)** — the Home widget grid now has three independent layout-mode buckets driven by `computeLayoutMode(isPortrait, width)` in `src/lib/layout-config.ts`: **phone** (portrait AND width < 700px `PHONE_MAX_WIDTH`) = single-column vertical stack (`grid grid-cols-1`); **tablet** (portrait AND 700–1279px `TABLET_MAX_WIDTH` — covers iPad mini 768/744, iPad Pro 12.9″ 1024, Nest Hub 1024) = uniform 2-column pairing grid (`grid grid-cols-2 gap-6`) where every widget is `col-span-1` and pairs up two per row (a lone odd widget leaves an empty half-row), with the This Week footer card spanning both columns (`homeFooterSpanClass("tablet")` = `col-span-2`); `TABLET_COL_SPANS` / `toTabletOrder` / `TABLET_FULL_WIDTH` removed — the tablet bucket is a plain order; **desktop** (landscape OR width ≥ 1280px) = an auto-fit tiling grid — `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(360px, 1fr))` fits as many uniform columns as the viewport holds, all widgets visible at once, vertical scroll; `WIDGET_WIDTHS` deleted; `widgetSpanClass("desktop")` returns `""`; `homeFooterSpanClass("desktop")` = `col-span-full`; `tabletSpan(index, count)` stretches the lone last widget of an odd tablet count to `col-span-2`; every Home card now fills its grid row (`h-full`) so short cards (Ask Consuela) match their row partner's height. Storage is **v3**: `{ phone: { widgets }, tablet: { widgets }, desktop: { widgets } }` in `consuela-home-layout` localStorage, written as plain JSON via `saveLayoutConfig` (no version constant). `loadLayoutConfig` migrates automatically: legacy v1 `{ widgets }` maps into all three buckets (tablet = the source order as-is); v2 `{ portrait, landscape }` maps phone=portrait, desktop=landscape, tablet = the phone order; v3 app-written configs round-trip **exactly** (no self-heal reorder on the v3 path — id validation only). `useOrientation` (`src/hooks/useOrientation.tsx`) resolves the live bucket SSR-safely (SSR defaults to desktop; listeners on the portrait media query + resize) and returns `{ orientation: LayoutMode; mounted }`; the old `Orientation` type name is fully removed. Pre-mount/SSR renders use `HOME_GRID_FALLBACK` (`grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3`) so there is no layout flash while the hook resolves. Settings → "Layout & display" gained 📱 Phone / 📱 Tablet / 🖥️ Desktop tabs to reorder each bucket independently; the reset toast says "phone, tablet, and desktop"; the JSON export includes all three; the contract is encoded in `tests/unit/layout-config.test.ts` (20 tests, updated for the uniform 1×1 sizes); a collapsed morning-briefing card still renders nothing (no hollow grid cell) | **Widget cards: WCAG contrast pass + standardized padding** — all widget-card text now meets 4.5:1 in dark + light (card-scoped text tokens, accessible accent variant for links/chips/badges, accent-button fills for white-text states, fog-proof schedule-row scrim); card padding standardized (headers/body/footer p-5 pl-14, stat tiles p-4) | **Pastel bento widget cards** — every Home widget (briefing, quick-ask, suggestions, leaderboard, today, schedule, current meal, tasks, this-week, stat tiles) got the weather-family design: per-card pastel identity gradient over dark glass, tone glow border, and a protruding top-left icon with halo; desktop Home is now a 3-column bento (weather spans 3, suggestions 2) with a single-column mobile stack | **Weather widget: protruding top-left icon + larger, calmer motion** — the animated weather icon overhangs the card's top-left corner (~24px out, scaled to 96px) with a slow-pulsing season-accent glow halo + drop shadow; card grew (min-height 220px, 60px temp) and all widget motion was slowed ~2× | **Summer weather visuals:** realistic layered palm silhouettes + accent-orange temperatures on a frosted panel (weather widget) | **Consuela integration (consuela-integration branch):** proactive suggestions feed ("Consuela suggests" Home card + /suggestions page), morning briefing card at the top of Home (7am cron), chat tools that really write (native OpenAI tool-calling — tasks/grocery/events persist to PB), unified daily chat thread mirrored from Telegram (Consuela Mirror bot, 5-min poll), Google Calendar auto-sync cron (every 5 min via host crontab + CRON_SECRET). | **User profile + task claiming (user-feature branch):** tap-your-avatar profile sheet on Home (emoji picker with categories, photo upload resized to 256px webp, PIN change, sign out), server-side PIN-verified `/api/members/*` routes, server-authoritative universal-task claiming (`/api/tasks/claim` — no more double-claim races), "Up for grabs 🤝" task filter, "My Tasks" now includes claimable universal tasks, universal recurring tasks regen unclaimed at week start, deleted signed-in member auto-logs out. | **Kitchen overhaul (kitchen-overhaul branch):** real calendar weeks (`weekOf` on `meal_plan_entries`), recipe-as-reference (`recipeId` + `resolveMealEntry` propagation), dedicated `/meals/archive` route with restore, family-scoped meals/pantry/grocery (all users see the same plan), auto grocery consolidation with accumulation + dedupe, pantry-aware recipe suggestions (`recipe-pantry-match.ts`), recipe import via fetch+Readability (no Playwright), unified types (`src/types/meals.ts` canonical), async PB reads in all hooks (fixed sync-cache race), PB auth requires env vars (no hardcoded password), `pod/sead.mjs` unified as tsx wrapper.
- **Grocery list = the 🛒 Shop step of /meals (kitchen-ui branch):** one grocery list for adding, checking off, and syncing; checked items have a per-row 🥫 Pantry action and a bulk "Send N to pantry" bar (sticky on mobile, section card on desktop) that moves bought items into the pantry as Plenty with an 8s Undo banner; Quick Add is its own card with category pills + already-added ✓ state; "Add missing from meal plan" is preview-first via the SyncPreviewSheet.
- **Last major UI refresh:** 2026-07-17 — Interaction consistency (Stream C): unified `.tap` / `.tap-sm` tap language (press 0.97, hover lift, focus ring) across all tappable surfaces.
- **Active integrations:** Meals ↔ Pantry ↔ Grocery bidirectional sync (`mealSyncService`), Emergency SMS + Email (free Gmail + carrier gateways), AI Chat ("Ask Consuela" — native tool-calling; chat tools write tasks/grocery/events to PB), **Google Calendar** (real API, Device Flow, encrypted **per-calendar** tokens/state in PB, **multi-calendar selection in Settings** — primary-only by default, **auto-sync cron every 5 min** + manual Sync now) + **Google Tasks + Reminders** wired but paused (Google's Device Flow allowed-scopes list doesn't include the Tasks API), **Consuela cron jobs** (suggestions / briefing / calendar-sync / telegram-poll — host crontab hits `POST /api/cron/consuela/*` with `CRON_SECRET` bearer), **new Home widgets** ("Consuela suggests" card + /suggestions page, Morning briefing card), **Telegram mirror** (Consuela Mirror bot mirrors group messages into the same daily dashboard chat thread), full theme system (3 modes + **10 accent colors** + high-contrast), **Home Assistant** (WebSocket bridge + optional Zigbee2MQTT MQTT tap, entity state cached in PB ha_entities)
- **IMPORTANT BUILD NOTE:** After every `npm run build`, the CSS chunks can go out of sync (Next.js 16.2.6 Turbopack bug). If the dashboard loads with broken layout (big icons, wrong nav styles), **restart the container**: `docker restart consuela-dashboard`. This is faster than a rebuild.
- **Navigation model:** Floating bottom-center glass capsule nav (Home, Ask, Meals, Tasks, Calendar, House, Settings) where the active item expands into a neon-lime pill, plus always-visible floating red Emergency shield button on Home
- **Deploy runbook (LOCAL ONLY — gitignored, contains NAS SSH credentials):**
`DEPLOY_NAS_LOCAL.md` in this repo root. Read it before any deploy/update of the live dashboard.
It covers: SSH access to the QNAP, tar-over-ssh sync (never rsync), docker build requirements
(--build-arg NEXT_PUBLIC_PB_URL), container swap, rollback twin, PB lockdown command, and 8
verified gotchas. **Tech surface for ops:** Next.js 16 + React 19 + Tailwind CSS 4, PocketBase-backed database (`pb-db.ts` + `db/index.ts`), API routes under `src/app/api/`, custom SVG animations (no framer-motion)
- **PocketBase migration:** **Complete (2026-06-18).** All 8 phases of the localStorage→PB migration have been implemented. PB is now the primary data source for members, events, schedules, grocery, pantry, meals, recipes, tasks, rewards, penalties, week data, family goals, hall of fame, emergency contacts, and auth sessions. localStorage is retained as a per-device cache/fallback. A global `CacheRefresher` component (in `layout.tsx`) polls PB every 60s and on visibility change to keep cross-device state in sync. The PB seed defines 18 collections total (6 original + 12 new).
